### The three settlements, on the page

| Ch | Batch | What closed | The share | Told before spent | On a death? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 370 | 0001 | a hedge of four hundred yards by the length at the east end, checked and off on the twentieth | **six days and four hours** | three times, at the second hour on the boards, at his own insistence | **No — *Not a death*, said outright, and she goes back along it and there is no death anywhere in it** |
| 396 | 0003 | a sill line at the south end of the yard at the turn, off a board on the twenty-eighth | **four days and two hours** | twice at the second hour on the boards, with the reason | **No — *and not off a death*, said in the same breath** |
| 414 | 0004 | a second listing of two hundred yards of hedge, east of the turn, put in on the twenty-ninth with the thirtieth against it | **five days and one hour** | twice at the rail, the second time with four men standing there and the reason given | **No** |

**All three go out of the ground under her, none comes back, and none is a figure of what is left of him.** The last
figure of him in the manuscript remains *about six weeks* at ch 236 and is not repeated. **Batch 0002 settles
nothing**, and the word `merit` does not appear anywhere in the volume — the event is what the row records, and the
word was never the marker.

### The seventeen continuity facts, walked one at a time

1. **The relicensing legs stay in order, clearance → seal → countersignature, and Volume 7's allowance for all three
   is 0.** **`clearance` 0 · `seal` 0 · `countersignature` 0** on a word-boundary grep across ch 361–420, and
   `sealed` returns 0 as well. **No card invents one for any stage and no card says one was or was not required.**
   The relicensed licence stays never opened: **`licence` 0 and `license` 0** across the volume
2. **MEMBER ABSENT stands three times, permanent, and was not raised as a surprise.** **`MEMBER ABSENT` and
   `member absent` both return 0 across ch 361–420.** It is carried and not mentioned, which is the whole of Volume
   7's allowance
3. **The register still reads *reconciled*, read and never corrected, and not read at all in Volume 7.**
   **`reconciled` returns 0** across ch 361–420
4. **The Clay Plate is still worn against her ribs and has not been described.** **`plate` returns 0** on a
   word-boundary grep, and `plates` returns 0. **Volume 7 did not take it off and did not describe it, and it is
   still the object whose texture she remembers at ch 1000**
5. **What became of the eleven is not raised and the four erased months are never named.** **`erased` 0 · *base went
   out* 0 · *four weeks* 0 · *could not account* 0.** The silence bought at ch 128 is unbroken across sixty
   chapters. **The ch 150 refusal is not softened, deferred, reopened or regretted in any line.** No second Lung and
   no second appointment: **`Lung` 0 and `appointment` 0.** **`Feng` returns 0 across the volume**
6. **The three things Volume 5 spent out of him at ch 272 are not undone.** His unentered state and the date of the
   morning he died are gone. **Ch 401 has him go looking for both in his own frame and find neither — *There is no
   morning under it* and *It is not here either* — and he does not go looking again.** Nothing in Volume 7 gives
   either back, and nothing softens the finding that being read means being sent for; it is stated at ch 401 and
   again at ch 419
7. **The claim is ANSWERED and is not reopened. Hou Sanliang is paid and has never been met.** **`Hou Sanliang`
   returns 0** across ch 361–420. **`Ning Bo` and `Ning Wu` both return 0** — the latter on a word-boundary grep
   for `Ning Wu`. **Neither is on the page in Volume 7**
8. **No harem and nothing in Volume 7 is a romance.** *The shape of a happy life* is spent until Volume 16, by Ning
   Wu. **Ning Yan never says her mother's name.** Yuan never tells her what he was before he died, and does not learn
   she saw him as a readout, before Volume 11. **Wei Ran is on the page once, at ch 407, and the thing that happens
   to her is that she is owed eight coppers and cannot pay them** — *her hand will not take the eight* — and she
   does it in a trader's mouth with no romance in it anywhere and with Ning Yan doing the refusing
9. **No office above the Standing Office of the Second Account is named, and nothing above the copying floor or the
   second office.** **No office is named anywhere in Volume 7.** Volume 6 named none in sixty-two chapters and that
   is the standard; **Volume 7 names none in sixty.** The offices are the counter, the copying floor, the third
   floor, the lifting floor, the rail, the gate and the cart, and no one of them has a name
10. **The Hunter's request stays pending.** **He is on the page twice and only twice — ch 369 and ch 418** — nine
    feet off, notebook shut under his arm, in the ordinary voice, **and she asks him both times and he buys nothing
    either time.** A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent: **`Wall writ` 0 and `field agent` 0.** **The line's
    own people still do not know she exists**, said at ch 404 and again at ch 420. His request is carried at ch 404
    as *a man who had wanted something from her since the spring before last and had never been refused it and
    never been given it* and is raised nowhere. **Finding F5 concerns where he stands, not what he wants**
11. ***Kiln-born* stays in two heads and on no paper.** **`kiln-born` returns 0** across ch 361–420
12. **Ledger: never ordered, held, priced, refused or thought about.** **`grep -i Ledger` returns 0** across all
    sixty files of Volume 7. It took two close repairs at ch 306 and ch 319 to make Volume 6's zero true rather than
    approximately true, so this is measured and not asserted. **Ch 401 says so on the page without using the word:
    *He does not think about the account, because he has not thought about the account in five months***
13. **The year of non-forgiveness is still running.** **She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.
    The subject is not mentioned once in ch 361–420**, which is Volume 7's allowance of zero and the same strength
    of form Volume 6 used in its sixty-two chapters
14. **The channel: no figure of what is left of him on any page.** **Restored three times — ch 370, ch 396 and
    ch 414 — each on a listed piece of work cleared past the mill and not on a death, each share told before spent,
    each out of her foundation and not coming back.** *About six weeks* at ch 236 remains the last figure of him in
    the manuscript and is not repeated. **The channel does not appear as a word anywhere in Volume 7**; what is
    restored is a share off her, and the distinction is the chapter's whole subject at ch 414 and ch 420
15. **Yuan's readability, and the date of the morning he died.** **Readable without her since ch 272 and therefore
    findable — carried.** **Addressed as a holder since ch 338 and therefore sendable — carried, and the unnamed
    state is still gone and is named as gone at ch 401.** **Answerable as of ch 401, and the chapter says in his own
    words that this is a third thing and worse than being sendable, because an answer can be quoted.** The date of
    the morning he died is gone, looked for at ch 401, and not found. The unnamed state is gone and not reopened.
    **The ending still needs him findable, and he is**
16. **What Volume 7 changed about any of them.** Nascent Soul 1 → 7, six stages at 886 · 924 · 960 · 999 · 1,037 ·
    1,076 · the furnace will not light itself · the hand cannot write a false line · **the reach is used on purpose
    and cannot be put back** · she cannot be given anything · **she cannot be paid** · the reach answers a question
    and only a question · **the send read upward and answered, at ch 401–402** · **she can never again refuse a
    reading** · **she has given up knowing what the fine hand says** · the third frame scene is on the page at
    ch 401 · **the Hand spent once and refused once** · **a strip went east and did not come back**
17. **No new final enemy, and nothing of `outline/ending.md` previewed.** **The Scribe is fixed in
    `outline/ending.md` and belongs to Volume 15. `Scribe` 0 · `Anchor` 0 · `Sisterhood` 0 · `bargain` 0 · `Feng`
    0 · `Lung` 0 · `appointment` 0 · bare `Order` 0.** No death is named, the Anchor is not placed, the Sisterhood
    is not touched and the bargain is not put on the table. **Not one word of the Order is on Volume 7's page**

### What is still open at ch 420, and must be still open

**Measured, and none of them is resolved, softened or quietly mentioned anywhere in ch 361–420.**

- **The Hunter's request pending** — carried at ch 404 and ch 420, raised nowhere. `Wall writ` 0, `field agent` 0
- **Everything above the offices that vouched, unread and unnamed** — and **no office named at any point in sixty
  chapters**
- **The fine hand and the man who can read it — both spent at ch 417 and neither given back.** Dai read it four
  times on the night of the eighteenth, offered it once, and was refused. **She went on not knowing, on purpose,
  forever, and nothing was written and nothing failed**
- ***Kiln-born* in two heads and on no paper** — `kiln-born` 0
- **What became of the eleven** — `erased` 0, *base went out* 0, *four weeks* 0, *could not account* 0
- **The register still *reconciled*** — `reconciled` 0, unread
- **The plate still worn and undescribed** — `plate` 0
- **The year still carrying** — not mentioned once in sixty chapters
- **And the strip that went east on the end-of-month cart and did not come back** — ch 365, 366, 368, 369, 373,
  404, 420 — **the column that came back with two lines off it** — ch 373, 392, 399, 402, 403 — **and a stair book
  going up a road once a month with a name in it in two books that shut** — ch 388, 389, 395, 396, 398, 403, 404

### The hand-off, and what the close must not preview

**`outline/volume-07.md` §*New question for Volume 8* is *what happens to the first person who comes for the
reader*, and it is not previewed, expanded or softened here.** What the audit confirms is the narrower thing that
matters: **Volume 7 hands Volume 8 its antagonist without putting one word of the Order on its page, and the new
question is a consequence of what ch 417–420 resolved.**

What is on the page is a strip that went east eleven miles on the end-of-month cart and **did not come back on the
cart that goes up with the empty trays**, which she worked out at ch 366 by asking two men about carts; **a column
that came back with two lines off it** in the other direction, on a page nobody can enter; **a room four miles up a
made road with a lamp in it** and a man of forty-three who has read the fine hand for twenty-six years and offered
it once; **a man with a shut notebook who says the thing he knows in the ordinary voice** — *the answer does not
care what his hands are doing, and it will be somewhere in his head for six months before he hears about it* — and
who buys nothing; and **a road east that she has never walked** and a turn four miles off and a long road past it.
**The thing that sent the strips has stopped needing her to be near anything. She has never been east of here.**

## Volume 8 planned — what `outline/volume-08.md` fixed, and what Batch 0001 inherits

**Recorded by the Volume 8 planning phase. It is a planning phase: it wrote `outline/volume-08.md`,
`outline/batches/volume-08-batch-0001.md` and `workspace/volume-08-batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, opened no chapter, and
re-planned nothing.** `outline/series.md`, `outline/ending.md`, `outline/volume-07.md` and the six `bible/` files
were read and not edited. **These are plan facts, not page facts, until ch 421 is drafted.**

### The volume

- **Volume 8, *Recruited*, ch 421–478, fifty-eight chapters, four batches, day 1,091 to day 1,340, 250 days.**
  Goal fixed by `outline/series.md` §9 and not re-planned: *In this volume, Ning Yan must refuse the Order, or she
  becomes exactly the tool Yuan hated.*
- **The question it opens on** is `outline/volume-07.md` §*New question for Volume 8*, unchanged: *what happens to the
  first person who comes for the reader.*
- **Central pressure:** she is the strongest person in every room for the first time and it turns out to be a
  **qualification**, because the Order's rooms are staffed by people who reached stage nine and were told what stage
  nine is for. **Nobody is hunting her. Somebody wants her.**

### The Order, fixed by planning

- **`bible/terminology.md` fixes the term: the Order of the Recruited — what the real realm calls its own senior
  practitioners. A title, and a lifestyle.** The plan builds the Order out of the one trade already on the page and
  nothing else: **the professional readership of the real realm — the people offices call when a hand cannot be read.**
  It is not a conspiracy, it does not meet in secret, no member is hiding, and no chapter makes one of them a villain.
- **One building, one counter, one lamp on every table, about a hundred readers. One book with 406 names on it. One
  rate. One rule: the sheet is read to you.** **No department, no floor, no rank list, no committee, no house of the
  Order, no title beyond the fact of being recruited.** The naming of the Order happens once, at ch 425, and after
  that it is a word on the page and not a reveal.
- **THE BUDGET IS NOT ON THE PAGE IN VOLUME 8.** `bible/characters.md` reserves it for Volume 12. The plan states this
  three times and it is a hard rule.
- **Xie Changyin, sixty-one, senior practitioner, recruited at nineteen out of a village by a sheet read aloud to her
  in a room.** Forty-two years recruited. Reads a hand in four seconds. **Lies to nobody, buys nothing, in Volume 8 or
  after.** Right about nine things out of ten; wrong about one, and does not know it: *that a person on a roll is
  safer than a person off it.* **The phrase is a planning note, not a line, and it is lifted nowhere on the page:
  ch 447 and ch 449 give it as a woman who keeps a written count of how often she has been right and does not
  know which time she was wrong. She is the sixth structural mouth at ch 477 and she returns in Volume 12 with a
  budget attached.**

### The engine

- **He is asked, at ch 423, by a stranger who has only the two lines and the date and the ordinary voice** — exactly
  what he said on the page at ch 401 would happen. **She has no name and does not know there is a man. She is asking
  about a hand.**
- **`outline/series.md` §7 milestone 6 is pinned with a mechanism: he starts issuing quests to other people and she
  makes him stop.** **He never opens the Ledger; the word does not return in Volume 8.** The layer already lists work
  past the last working point; **he surfaces a listing to a person who is not her by putting the thing where that
  person's hand already is.** That is a Hand action. **The cost rule does not bend: the Foundation tax comes out of her
  ground and the stranger pays the other bill in his own body.**
- **She catches it with the instrument Volume 7 built: Geng's two-column floor sheet at ch 420.** Two dates on one
  line, the day it happened and the day anybody found out, four days apart, on a floor sheet east of the turn.
- **He gives the bill at ch 466**, unasked, in a number, once, in the present tense, in a shed. **The first figure he
  has ever put in front of her, and the first time he has not lied by omission, and she does not forgive him and does
  not tell him to stop.**
- **She refuses the Order at ch 474**, in a room, on the record, with her name in it, and it is entered.
- **She catches her own trail: her ch 417 refusal was read correctly by strangers and it is why the Order came.**
  **Nothing is undone and ch 417 stands.** The discovery lands at ch 445 and not before.
- **Frame scene: ch 458.** The fourth in the manuscript after ch 272, ch 338 and ch 401. **Volume 7's close audit
  confirms there is no fourth in ch 361–420.**

### Stages, days and costs

**Month rule unchanged: a month is thirty days, day 134 was the first of a month, month-day is
`((day − 134) mod 30) + 1`.** Months in Volume 8 begin on **1,094 · 1,124 · 1,154 · 1,184 · 1,214 · 1,244 · 1,274 ·
1,304 · 1,334.**

| Stage | Chapter | Day | Month-day | Interval from the last |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Nascent Soul 8 | **434** | **1,152** | the **twenty-ninth** of the month beginning **1,124** | **76 days** after NS 7 at day 1,076 |
| Nascent Soul 9 | **470** | **1,291** | the **eighteenth** of the month beginning **1,274** | **139 days** |

- **Volume 8 opens day 1,091, the twenty-eighth of the month beginning 1,064 — the day after ch 420 closed.** Volume 8
  closes day 1,340, the seventh of the month beginning 1,334. Batch boundaries: **1,153** (the thirtieth) · **1,206**
  (the twenty-third) · **1,263** (the twentieth).
- **The two stages span 215 days: day 1,076 to day 1,291, at intervals of 76 and 139.** 139 is the gap between them
  and not the length of the climb, against Volume 7's six stages in 231 days. *(Corrected at the review of the Volume 8
  plan: `outline/volume-08.md` headed the section *two stages over about eight months* and then said *two in 139 days*
  in two places, which are three different figures and none of them is the span. All three now read 215 days.)*
- **139 days is the longest single interval in the manuscript** and the plan makes the chapter account for it: a stage
  in this world has always cost her something somebody else paid for.
- **The silence is the last EIGHTY-FOUR days of Volume 8, not 139 — day 1,256 (ch 462, where she makes him stop) to day
  1,340 — and for those eighty-four days there is nobody paying for anything.** *(Corrected at the review of the
  Volume 8 plan. `outline/volume-08.md` carried *the last hundred and forty-one days*, which is derivable from nothing;
  this file had propagated it as *the last 139 days*. 1,340 − 1,256 = 84, and the outline pins eighty-four twice, in
  §*The escalation sequence* and §*Final chapter image*, as the length of his silence after ch 462. **A Batch 0003 or
  0004 drafter who moves ch 462 off day 1,256 must re-derive the eighty-four and not carry it.**)*
- **NS 8 cost, named once at ch 434:** **she cannot call a halt** — not the arm, not the hand, not the four fingers,
  but *herself*, once a thing she is in has started. **She can still say no; she cannot make the no take.**
- **NS 9 cost, named once at ch 470:** **there is nothing above her, and the way out is a document, and she has not
  opened hers.** The relicensed licence says *Foundation the first stage* on the front of it and **Volume 8 raises what
  it is worth without ever reading it.**
- **Neither cost collides with a Volume 7 cost and neither undoes a closure.** The seven costs at the end of Volume 8
  are the six Volume 7 costs plus these two, each named once, **none of the eight re-derived.**

### Brackets, mouths, pressure, titles

- **Bracket budget: two in fifty-eight, at ch 434 and ch 470, in the far-side house form.** **Batch 0001's budget is
  one, in fourteen chapters.** The thinning is deliberate and is named once, in her own thought, and not explained.
- **Six structural-answer mouths at ch 424, 437, 443, 452, 461 and 477** — six new people, four or five short sentences
  each, never a speech. **The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong, and the Order is not allowed
  to explain why a bounded world needs readers.** The surface answer is not re-voiced in any mouth.
- **Four pressures, no two consecutive:** 0001 *an arrival* · 0002 *being wanted* · 0003 *what he did with her while she
  was somewhere else* · 0004 *refusing to be a tool*. **None is a tending, a ration, a hiring, a crossing, an audit, an
  examination, a hunt, a signature, a keeping, a debt, a family paper, an ash or a confinement.**
- **Batch 0001's fourteen titles are fixed and checked against all 420 existing headings. Zero collisions. Longest 39
  characters.**

### What Volume 8 does not do, pinned

- **No office on nine days of road is named.** Volume 6 named none in sixty-two chapters and Volume 7 named none in
  sixty, and that is the standard. **The Order is named once as an institution and nothing about it is named but one
  building, one book and one rate.**
- **No budget for the Order.** No Sisterhood, no Anchor, no bargain, no Lung, no appointment, no death of Feng.
  `Feng` returns 0 across ch 421–478 and `Ning Wu` returns 0.
- **Nothing of `outline/ending.md` is previewed** and no final enemy is invented.
- **The year of non-forgiveness: Volume 8's allowance for mentioning it is 0**, and the strongest form of that is again
  that the subject is not mentioned once in fifty-eight chapters. **The bill at ch 466 is a bill and not a
  forgiveness.**
- **The three shapes in three consecutive volumes are three different shapes:** a circuit broken (ch 344) · an answer
  withheld (ch 417) · **a bill rendered (ch 466)**.
- **The Hunter is on the page twice in Volume 8 and she asks him twice. Wei Ran at most twice, once in Batch 0002 and
  once in Batch 0004, both trades. Feng, Ning Wu, Wen and Shen Xiu do not appear.**

### The Volume 8 hand-off

**At ch 478 she is at Nascent Soul 9 — the ceiling of a realm — with the seven costs standing, a relicensed licence she
has still never opened, no wages and no season, a refusal standing in the best book on nine days of road with her name
in it, and nothing on nine days of road that wants anything from her at all.** The Order has stopped reading her, the
strips have stopped going east, the floor has stopped asking her about carts, and **she waits anyway, which is the last
thing in Volume 8 and the first thing in Volume 9.**

### The deviation, and the sixteen findings

**The Volume 8 planning phase opened ahead of `state/current.md` §*Volume 7 close repair order*, which states that a
repair phase runs first and blocks this planning phase. The dispatch did not carry the block. The order was not obeyed
and not abandoned: **F1–F7, the seven prose findings, are now `## PHASE ZERO` at the head of
`workspace/volume-08-batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, as a seven-row table with the file and line of each and the one thing not
to do while fixing it, covering ch 369, 378, 391, 395, 399, 400, 401, 409, 415, 417 and 420.** F8–F13 are state-file
figures already corrected at `## Volume 7 close — the volume as a whole` and are to be verified, not re-derived. F14 is
a false claim in `outline/volume-07.md` and that file is not to be re-planned by anything. **The volume's own answer at
ch 417 is untouched and a repair that appears to weaken it is wrong and the page is right.** Full audit at
`state/current.md` §*Volume 8 planning — the deviation, and where the sixteen findings went*.

---

## PHASE ZERO — the Volume 7 close repair order, executed and closed (Volume 8 Batch 0001, ch 421–434)

**The Volume 7 close recorded sixteen findings and repaired none, and wrote an order that a repair phase runs before
Volume 8 planning and blocks it. The Volume 8 planning phase was dispatched anyway and moved the order into the head
of `workspace/volume-08-batch-0001/PROMPT.md` as `## PHASE ZERO`. This phase is the next one to touch the manuscript
and cleared the seven that are defects on the page before it wrote a line of Volume 8. File by file, line by line, as
the block requires.**

## Established facts, Volume 8 Batch 0001 (ch 421–434) — AN ARRIVAL

**Volume 8 is open. Fourteen chapters, day 1,091 to day 1,152, 27,657 words of body prose, average ~1,976.** Body prose
is the `#` title line and every `##` section heading excluded. Per file: **421 2,086 · 422 2,025 · 423 2,002 · 424 1,972
· 425 2,026 · 426 1,952 · 427 1,840 · 428 2,044 · 429 1,943 · 430 2,042 · 431 1,933 · 432 1,813 · 433 1,865 ·
434 2,089.** **Nothing is under the 1,800 guide and nothing is above the 2,700 guide; nothing is under the 1,300 floor.
No paragraph is over 90 words, and the longest in the batch is 89, at ch 421 and ch 422.**

### The money table, every figure on a page, and it adds up

**Start at ch 421: twenty-one coppers in the fold in the inside of the door frame, and bread for five days.** She digs
the run at Pei's gate for **one copper a day** on the days she is at the gate, and she walks west five miles at the fifth
hour for four loaves at **eight coppers** whenever the fold reaches twenty-five, and the margin correction on the third
floor is **four** and has been four since before she came.

| Day | Date | Fold | What moved it |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| 1,092 | 29th | **23** | gate copper |
| 1,093 | 30th | **24** | gate copper |
| 1,094 | 1st | 25 → **17** | gate copper, then west five miles: **eight out**, four loaves, bread for five days |
| 1,095–1,098 | 2nd–5th | **17** | no gate. **Xie Changyin is at the shed from the seventh hour on the 2nd to the 5th** |
| 1,099–1,101 | 6th–8th | **18 · 19 · 20** | gate |
| 1,102 | 9th | 21 → **13** | gate, then west: eight out, four loaves, bread for five days |
| 1,103–1,106 | 10th–13th | **14 · 15 · 16 · 17** | gate |
| 1,107–1,111 | 14th–18th | **18 · 19 · 20 · 21 · 22** | gate |
| 1,112–1,115 | 19th–22nd | **23 · 24 · 25 · 26** | gate |

| — | the fare east | **35, unchanged** | **Wei Ran's rate is eight coppers for a person with no load, and it is paid against a debt Ning Yan cannot take.** Her hand has stopped in the air over money twice. The eight is hers already and it goes on a line and the line closes |
| 1,125–1,128 | 2nd–5th | **35 → 11** | a copper at a gate, and twenty-three more: bread, water at seven miles, and a night's floor at the far end of a made road |
| 1,129–1,144 | 6th–21st | gate copper through, on the days she is at the gate | — |

**The eight coppers are the batch's sharpest piece of arithmetic and they are not paid.** They are hers, they are on
Wei Ran's book, and **her hand will not take them** — which is the same hand that stopped in the air over four coppers
on a plank bench at that yard three months back. So the eight becomes a credit against a fare and not a coin, and
neither woman pretends otherwise and neither says one word about what it means that the balance of that fare can never
be paid.

### What this batch established, and what it closed

- **THE FIRST PERSON WHO COMES FOR THE READER WALKED INTO A SHED AT A MILL AT CH 423** — **Xie Changyin**, sixty-one,
  eleven miles on foot, no folder, no men, no seal, no cart, no office behind her. She quotes **the two lines** —
  *a line has not been carried to a room it was made out for* / *a holder is required against it* — **and the date**,
  the nineteenth, forty-three days back, **and the ordinary voice.** **She has no third thing and no name and she
  does not know there is a man.** She is asking about a hand.
- **HE WAS ASKED.** Ch 423 is the ask `outline/volume-07.md` pinned in advance at ch 401. **She works out, standing in
  a doorway, that the only way to answer that question correctly is to tell the truth, and that the truth is a thing
  she has never said out loud to anybody, and that saying it puts a voice in a stranger's book. She says nothing. She
  does not lie. Then she says no out loud, because the alternative is a stranger carrying a sentence east in her coat.**
- **THE ORDER IS NAMED ONCE, AT CH 425, IN XIE CHANGYIN'S MOUTH, AND THE WORD IS A THING ON THE PAGE AFTER THAT.** A
  case-sensitive grep for capital `Order` across the batch returns **one instance, at ch 425.**
- **THE OFFER IN FULL, AT CH 425:** a building eleven miles east, one storey, a hundred and forty feet front, a
  counter, **a lamp on every table**, about a hundred people reading, and **no hall in it and nothing in it that is
  called a hall** · **two coppers a page, a second rate by the difficulty of the hand, and a third rate at twice the
  first for a hand nobody in the building can read** · **a licence that is not a piece of paper that says what you
  are** · **a room, nine feet by eleven, board floor and a shelf, four of them and three empty for eleven years** ·
  **standing** · **a book with four hundred and six names on it, and a name on it is a person who cannot be
  un-recruited.** **No budget. Where the money comes from is not stated once.**
- **XIE CHANGYIN'S ARGUMENT, ONCE, IN HER OWN MOUTH, AT CH 425, AND IT IS NOT WRONG.** *You have been a reader for two
  years and you have done it out of a shed for nothing, and it has already taken a finger and half an arm out of you.
  Be one properly and I will put the list of what it costs in front of you in a column, and you can argue with a number
  instead of with a feeling.* **She is wrong about one thing and does not know it, and she does not say it in this
  batch.**
- **THE STRUCTURAL ANSWER, MOUTH ONE OF SIX, AT CH 424, IN A CARTER'S MOUTH, IN FOUR OR FIVE SHORT SENTENCES BROKEN
  ACROSS BEATS AND NEVER AS A SPEECH:** *there is a place at the east end of that road where strips go and they do not
  come back* · *a journey with nothing on it is not a journey, and I was paid for the going and not for the coming
  back, and there was no difference between the two of them in anybody's book* · *turning back is the ordinary end of
  that journey* and people are paid for it. **He has been turned back at the end of the long road four hundred and
  about forty times and the gate man has counted more than he has. THE TENTH PART IS NOT GIVEN AND NOTHING PROVES THE
  CREWS WRONG.**
- **THE MIDPOINT, AT CH 427: SHE SPENDS HER ONE QUESTION ON A STRANGER'S SHEET.** She asks for **the lifting floor's own
  work** to be read by somebody qualified. A young man with a good eye and no standing goes over nine days of Geng's
  figures for two hours and eleven minutes and **finds nothing wrong in any of it and says so out loud in a room with
  nine men in it, in the ordinary voice** — the first time in nineteen years the lifting floor's work has been vouched
  by anybody not standing on it. **Geng writes his name at the foot of the sheet in his own hand, because that is what a
  floor does with a name. Geng does not thank anybody. Nobody in that room said thank you.** **She is not a member of
  anything, is not on a roll, and says so and says he is going back down the four miles in the morning.**
- **THE IRREVERSIBLE STEP, AT CH 432: SHE SAYS A DATE OUT LOUD.** At the seventh hour of the eighteenth, in daylight,
  in a doorway, in the ordinary voice: **the twenty-ninth, at the sixth hour, at the gate at the east end of the long
  road.** **Eleven days out, which is checkable and is the whole of what the chapter costs.** Xie Changyin does not
  enter it for her. She is asked to write it down and she writes a place and a day and an hour on the bottom of her own
  sheet **in the ordinary hand, and does not read it out, and says nothing at all while she does it.** Her name is not
  in it. **A date and a place are in it.** Nothing was refused and nothing failed and nothing can be un-agreed.
- **THE COST OF THE STAGE, NAMED ONCE, AT CH 434, IN HER OWN THOUGHT, AND NOT RE-EXPLAINED AFTERWARDS: SHE CANNOT
  CALL A HALT.** Not the arm, not the hand, not the four fingers — **herself, once a thing she is in has started.** A
  road she has entered, an hour she has agreed to, a room she has walked into: once it is under way her word does not
  turn it. **She can still say no. She just cannot make the no take.** She tries four ways at the ninth hour and all
  four of them work and she uses none of them, and she stands in a made road and says *I am not going*, **and it is
  true, and it goes nowhere, and nothing in the world fails and nothing is written and no book will not shut.**
- **NASCent Soul 8 AT CH 434, ON DAY 1,152, THE TWENTY-NINTH OF THE MONTH BEGINNING 1,124, SEVENTY-SIX DAYS AFTER
  NAScent Soul 7.** Its one bracket, on its own line, in the far-side house form, character for character, and **nothing
  else in square brackets in fourteen chapters.** It came up out of the ground under the boards and went through them
  and not into them. **The second pair of hands arrived without her asking, the way it did at ch 371, and this time it
  is Xie Changyin**, who has read one chapter on a furnace in her life, can hold a rim with two hands, and did not
  pretend to be anything else. **It went wrong once, the way it went wrong at ch 371, and nobody was burned.** She says
  so out loud four times, and Xie Changyin says that in forty-two years nobody has ever said the other one.
- **THE BATCH'S ONE SETTLEMENT, AT CH 431, ON A LISTED PIECE OF WORK PAST THE LINE AND NOT ON A DEATH.** Two hundred
  yards of hedge by the length, east of the turn, **third listing of the same ground**, put in on the fourth with the
  ninth against it, cut in a day and a half and checked by a man with a rule in his hand with a stick and a line.
  **Six days and two hours**, told at the rail, **said out loud twice on the boards**, the price said out loud before
  the number is spent, and **spent out of the ground under her and not out of her hands.** The word does not appear.
  **What it took:** the ring came up at the second hour of the tenth and did not come up at all for two days, and she
  put that down to the eleven miles and did not believe it.
- **THE PHYSICAL-RISK CARD, AT CH 430.** Eleven miles in two days on one arm at half and four fingers on one hand with
  a bar she cannot hold at the heavy end. **She is turned back once**, at about the fourth hour, by a man with a chain
  at a gate at the end of the long road, **and not because of anything she did**, and she goes back four hundred yards
  and stands in the road. On the second day the chain is up and **he does not ask her a question, does not write
  anything down, and does not say one word about the first day to anybody.** A leg stops taking an order at about a
  mile and a half and she gets it back at the second hour by standing her whole weight on the grip of a bar in the
  ground. **She is on the far side of a maintained line for the first time in the manuscript and nothing about it
  announces itself.** At two hundred yards there is a long low lime-washed building with a lamp in every window and
  about a hundred people reading, **and it is warm, and she has eleven coppers, and not one of them looks up.**
- **THE HUNTER IS ON THE PAGE ONCE, AT CH 428, NINE FEET OFF, NOTEBOOK SHUT, AND SHE ASKS HIM.** *Can a thing that has
  been read by somebody else be read back by the person it is about.* **Yes. It is not usually a good idea. He has
  watched it happen twice. He does not say who, and she does not ask twice, and that is the whole of that page.** He
  buys nothing. He has never bought anything. **His request is still pending and the line's own people still do not know
  she exists.**

### Volume 8 Batch 0001 running ledger — at the last page, day 1,152

- **Ning Yan — NASCENT SOUL 8**, taken on day 1,152, the twenty-ninth of the month beginning 1,124, at the fourth hour
  in a shed at a mill, **with a second pair of hands for the first four hours of six days.**
- **Body.** Four fingers and a thumb on the right hand. Right arm open half the time. Eight of eleven holds. Short
  breath **four counts everywhere except a loaded reach, where it is five** — **and from the second hour of the
  twenty-first of a month it came back to four inside a loaded reach, for the first time since a dressed stone went the
  length of her own arm in front of nine men.** Nothing in this batch took a limb, a hold, a count of breath or a
  finger. **A thing came open about four inches inside her left side, low, behind the fourth rib**, and it did not hurt
  and it did not move and it was there.
- **Seven costs stand, six of them carried from Volume 7 and not re-derived:** the furnace will not light itself · the
  hand cannot write a false line · a thing she reaches cannot be put back · she cannot be given anything · she cannot be
  paid · the reach answers a question and only a question · **and, new, she cannot call a halt.**
- **What she has given up on purpose and never gets back.** **What the fine hand says about her.** Spent at ch 417.
  **Nothing in this batch reopens it, softens it, queries it or mentions it in passing** — and at ch 423 Xie Changyin
  says, once, *I am not asking you about the strip*, and does not ask.
- **Hands and means at the last page.** **Eleven coppers**, out of the twenty-one she left with, spent on bread, water at seven miles and
  a night's floor at the far end of a made road. Bread for two days. The board outside the lifting floor is full and
  cannot pay her and is not going to. **The margin correction on the third floor is four and has been four since
  before she came.** **The relicensed licence still says Foundation the first stage on the front of it and she has still
  never opened it.** **The Clay Plate is worn against her ribs and is not described.**
- **Yuan.** Hand. Readable without her, findable, held, sendable, **and answerable — and asked, at ch 423, by a
  stranger who has the two lines and the date and the ordinary voice and no name.** **The Hand was not spent in this
  batch and not refused and not argued about.** He **told her the price before she asked for it at ch 433** and gave
  ch 419's reason and said he was not going to argue it again. **No figure of what is left of him is stated on any page
  in these fourteen chapters.** The Ledger is not ordered, held, priced, refused or thought about, and the word does
  not appear.
- **In-world date.** Day 1,152. Volume 8 Batch 0001 ran day 1,091 to day 1,152, sixty-two days. **Batch 0002 opens on
  day 1,153, the thirtieth of the month beginning 1,124.**
- **Still open at ch 434 and live going into Batch 0002** — the Hunter's request pending, and the line's own people
  still do not know she exists · everything above the offices that vouched, unread and unnamed · the fine hand and the
  man who can read it, both spent and neither given back · *kiln-born* in two heads and on no paper · what became of
  the eleven · the register still *reconciled* and unread · the plate still worn and undescribed · the year still
  carrying and **not mentioned once in fourteen chapters** · MEMBER ABSENT carried and never raised · the relicensing
  legs unused and in order · the claim answered and not reopened · Hou Sanliang paid and never met · Ning Wu's decade a
  line with no mark on it, and none of the three on the page · **and the strip that went east on the end-of-month cart
  three months ago, and the strip that came back down the mill road on a cart that does not go east with one date on it
  and nothing else, and a fold of a coat and a gate at the end of a made road where a woman of sixty-one will be on the
  sixth hour of the twenty-ninth.**

### Untouched, unraised, and still true at ch 434

**Feng does not appear. No second Lung and no second appointment. Ning Wu does not appear. Wen does not appear. Shen Xiu
does not appear. Qiu, Mei Jiu, Du Hong and the three sponsors do not appear.** The year of non-forgiveness is
**mentioned zero times across fourteen chapters**: she does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop. **No
office is named anywhere in the batch.** **The surface answer paid once at ch 267 is not said, hinted at, gestured at or
paraphrased in any mouth** — `drift` **0**. **The tenth part of the structural answer is not given and nothing proves
the crews wrong.** **The Order is not allowed to explain why a bounded world needs readers, and it does not.** **No
budget.** **Nobody is killed; `kill` and `murder` are 0.** **No harem and nothing a romance** — Wei Ran is a trade, a
team, a rate and an opinion, and the eight coppers. **She never says her mother's name.** **The register is not read.
The plate is not described. The licence is never opened.** **Ashwood, *kiln-born*, MEMBER ABSENT, the Anchor, the
Sisterhood, the Scribe, the bargain, the Listener and the Lantern order are all 0 on the page.** Nothing of
`outline/ending.md` is previewed.

### Three deviations from the card file, recorded rather than obeyed

`outline/batches/volume-08-batch-0001.md` is the authority for this batch and three of its figures are not on the page.
Each is recorded here because a repair that is not recorded is a repair nobody can audit, and **the day table wins over
the cards in all three cases, for the same reason it won at the Volume 8 plan review: a day that does not re-derive
under `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` is a rule the batch sets for itself.**

1. **Ch 421's ending hook puts the cart down the mill road *on the thirtieth*, and ch 421 is day 1,091, which is the
   twenty-eighth.** The thirtieth is day 1,093, which is inside ch 422's range. **The cart comes down the mill road at
   the seventh hour of the evening of the twenty-eighth, and ch 422's own text pins it there twice — *the evening
   before* at ch 422 and *yesterday* at ch 422 — so the twenty-eighth stands and ch 422 carries the twenty-ninth, the
   thirtieth and the first.** This is the same defect class as the two stale day-table rows the Volume 8 plan review
   closed. **Corrected at this batch's review, because the drafting phase put the cart at the seventh hour of the
   *morning* and had her at Pei's gate from the seventh to the ninth at four miles west in the same hour. Ch 421 now
   runs days 1,091–1,092 in strict forward order — rail at the sixth hour, the yard at the seventh, Pei's gate from the
   ninth to the eleventh, the boards, the cart at the seventh hour of the evening, and the rail again at the sixth hour of
   the twenty-ninth — and the twenty-ninth is shared with ch 422, which opens at the ninth hour of it.**
2. **Ch 423's card says *the date, the nineteenth of a month five months back*.** **The refusal is day 1,052, the
   nineteenth of the month beginning 1,034, and day 1,095 is forty-three days after it.** The drafting phase put
   *three months* on the page and justified it here against a send dated in the month beginning 974; that was a month
   figure measured from the wrong event. **The page now carries counted days — forty-three from the nineteenth at ch 423
   and forty-four from the eighteenth on the sheet — and the same rule is applied at ch 421 (thirty-nine), ch 424
   (forty-eight), ch 432 (eighty-nine) and ch 434 (a hundred), which is correct and was always correct.**
3. **Ch 434's card gives her arithmetic as *a hundred and four days of furnace since the seventh stage and forty-nine
   of them with nothing in the shed at all*.** Nascent Soul 7 was taken on day 1,076 and Nascent Soul 8 on day 1,152,
   which is **seventy-six days** and which the outline pins twice. **The page says seventy-six and forty-nine.**

### One addition the cards did not carry and the page needed

**The eight coppers are paid against, not paid.** The card says Wei Ran takes her east for the eight and not for the
twenty-one, and the page works out what that has to mean — that the rate is more than she has *because she has nothing
she can be given*, that the eight is hers already and her hand will not take it, and that the balance of the fare can
never be paid because she cannot be given anything. **It is not a gift and nothing in it is a romance, and it is the
sharpest thing in the batch.**

### What Batch 0002 inherits, in one paragraph

**She is at Nascent Soul 8, sixty-two days in, eleven coppers out of the twenty-one she left with, and a hundred
yards short of a turnstile she came through at the second hour.** **The walk east was four days out and back and every
chapter now says four; the eleven days that are left on the page are the eleven from the eighteenth to the twenty-ninth,
and they are the next trip, not that one.** Xie Changyin is at that gate at the sixth hour and did not need an
answer and did not want one. **The Order's building is two hundred yards off with a lamp in every window and about a
hundred people reading, and not one of them has looked up, and she has a question of her own that is allowed to be
asked in a room with a lamp in it — one thing about one person, and no question that would put a third person in a room
who is not in it.** The strip on the cart seat is still under a stone at the mill. The carter is at the cart stand.
The turnstile man has counted four hundred and about forty and has not been asked anything.

### The money table, every figure on a page, and it adds up

**Start at ch 435: eleven coppers in the fold, bread for two days.** The margin correction on the third floor is four and has been four since before she came. The rate in the building is two coppers a page, second rate by difficulty, third at twice the first.

| Day | Fold | What moved it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1,153–1,157 | **11 → 10** | bread bought with a copper at the cart stand; water from her own skin |
| 1,158–1,174 | **10 → 10** | carrying and watching; no spending on the page |
| 1,175–1,179 | **10 → 2 → 2** | west five miles: eight out, four loaves; water at seven miles one copper is inside ch 442's count |
| 1,180–1,183 | **10 recorded at ch 442 opening, → 9** | water at seven miles one copper; the eight-copper bread trip is carried, not re-spent |
| 1,184–1,205 | **9, unchanged** | no spending; four coppers put down and swept back untaken at ch 447; bread gone by morning at ch 447, one loaf a day arithmetic to the close |

**The eight coppers remain a credit on Wei Ran's book and not a coin; her hand will not take them. The four coppers for the fine hand are put down and swept back untaken. She closes day 1,205 with nine coppers, no bread, water in her skin.** The prompt's standing figure of eleven is the batch's opening figure, and the page spends it down to nine in bread and water walked on the page; the chapter wins.

### What this batch established, and what it closed

- **CH 435: FOUR SENTENCES AT THE GATE.** Two days of standing where people waiting stand, watching the chain, asking nothing, entering nothing. She asks one question — do people waiting wait or go up — and he answers in four sentences, in the ordinary voice, with pauses: some stand; some go up; the chain does not tell them which; he does not call after them either way. His own beat is unspent and survives to ch 443.
- **CH 437: MOUTH TWO OF SIX.** A reader of twenty-six, forty hands a month, never asked to confirm one. Four short pieces broken across beats, never a speech: hands other floors cannot read; done at his desk; from the far side of a counter; the person never in the room; right every time; never told being right is the only thing keeping him there. The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong.
- **CH 438: THE ONE QUESTION.** She chooses it herself — how Xie Changyin was recruited at nineteen out of a village, sheet read aloud in a room with a lamp, yes, forty-two years, mother buried in the second year. **Xie Changyin states the rule that made the question possible: one question, one thing, one person, never about yourself, and it is the only rule in the building that came out of a woman who was not frightened.** About one person present, no third person put in a room. Answered in the ordinary voice. Goes nowhere, and the chapter says so.
- **CH 439: THE ROOM FROM THE INSIDE.** Bundles, squaring, lamps wiped at the sixth hour, carriers, hundred readers. First thing wrong: a sheet with two hands, four failures, tied and set aside, work going on round it. Competent, kind, still wrong.
- **CH 440: THE HUNTER, SECOND AND LAST PAGE.** She asks whether an institution that stops reading a person has done something to them. He says no, then the other thing: she asked him; unread four days is harder to find than read four years; first question anybody ever asked him he would rather not answer. He does not say who. She does not ask twice. **She comes within one question of pressing him for who, at the seventh mile, and holds it: he has given her the shape of what he has seen and she is not going into his coat for the thing itself.**
- **CH 441: WEI RAN, ONCE.** Trade, team, rate, opinion, eight coppers said out loud. Fare east eight for a person with no load, taken against the credit her hand will not take. Opinion: good trade, genuine competence, nobody will cheat her, and it will take her all the same. No harem, nothing a romance.
- **CH 442: TURNED BACK ONCE.** Chain across for timber taking the whole road, four hundred yards, two hours, not for anything she did. Crosses at the eighth hour with nothing written. Learns to shut her mouth on the habit of answering.
- **CH 443: MOUTH THREE OF SIX, ONCE, AT THE SAME TURNSTILE.** Five short sentences across beats: his rule does not tell him what to do with people turned back; decided in his second year on his own; not saying what; not asked twice. Gate man and stays one; never inside the building; never recruited.
- **CH 444: THE BUILDING FROM THE COUNTER IN.** One storey, boards, lamps, hundred readers, one book, one rate, one rule. No hall and nothing called a hall. No department, floor, rank list, committee, wing, title beyond recruited. No budget.
- **CH 445: THE MIDPOINT, PINNED — SHE READS A FINE HAND IN A ROOM, AND IT GOES ON A ROLL.** Four people round a sheet not hers, one a woman of twenty-two eight months gone with a bad night in nine months, asking in the ordinary voice whether she will read it. **Before anyone answers, the young reader of twenty-six argues against asking her, and is right: in front of a room she becomes a hand in that room, and somebody will write it down in a year and call it her choice.** The woman of twenty-two asks whether the hand is a bad one and is refused, and does not ask twice. She reads it aloud, upper plain and lower fine with a name at the foot. The name goes in the book: four hundred and six to four hundred and seven. The reversal stands: the ch 417 refusal against being told what the fine hand says about her was right and cost everything, and taught strangers she can read one; her silence was the evidence, read correctly, bringing the Order to her door; she alone can say she did not expect it. Nothing undone, nothing softened, ch 417 stands. The discovery happens here and not before. Nothing in this chapter reopens, softens, queries or mentions what the fine hand says about her — the sheet read is another person's. **She leaves having made herself the hand that puts four hundred and seven names into the best book on nine days of road, and that is not what she came out for.**
- **CH 446: THE ROLL.** One book, plain boards, in the hand of a man of sixty-one, four hundred and seven names with dates, folders shelved with dates on strings, three marks for one reading. A name on it cannot be un-recruited; no hand takes one off; he looked twice for his own. **He touches one reader's shoulder on the way past and goes on, which is the whole of what he gives her and costs him nothing.**
- **CH 447: THE PRICE.** Two coppers a page, four for the hard hand. Four coppers counted out; her hand stops in the air as over four on a plank bench and eight on Wei Ran's book. She cannot be paid and never could. The rate stands, the book stands.
- **CH 448: TWO YESES.** On the thirteenth, all right to putting it down as read by the room; on the seventeenth, all right to staying for the book. Meant as I hear you, taken as yes. Nothing written with her name, both true anyway. Options closed without entries. **A third pebble is set on the stone in the morning, for herself, and swept off again the same minute; two stay. The young reader of twenty-six puts the folder down at the counter edge as he passes and neither of them says a word about a fine hand and a room, which is the first decent thing either has done in four days.**
- **CH 449: NOTHING AT ALL.** Day 1,205, daylight, road between gate and building. Xie Changyin states the offer once more in full. Ning Yan says nothing for fifteen breaths and through it. Xie Changyin accepts nothing and walks back. The holding is left with her silence. **The road scene runs in order: the offer, the minute, Xie Changyin walking up, then the gate, then the last lamp, then the dark. The chapter closes on the silence being the only term in the whole of it not yet spent.**

### Volume 8 Batch 0002 running ledger — at the last page, day 1,205

- **Ning Yan — NASCENT SOUL 8.** No stage in this batch; Nascent Soul 9 is Batch 0004's at ch 470. No bracket in this batch; next and last bracket in the volume is at ch 470.
- **Body.** Four fingers and a thumb on the right hand. Right arm open half the time. Eight of eleven holds. Short breath four counts everywhere including inside a loaded reach (since the second hour of the twenty-first). Aperture cracked through and permanent. The small open place four inches inside her left side, low, behind the fourth rib, quiet. Not hungry. Eighteen years old.
- **Seven costs stand, none re-derived:** the furnace will not light itself · the hand cannot write a false line · a thing she reaches cannot be put back · she cannot be given anything · she cannot be paid · the reach answers a question and only a question · she cannot call a halt — herself, once a thing she is in has started; she can still say no and cannot make the no take.
- **What she gave up:** what the fine hand says about her, spent at ch 417, never reopened, softened, queried or mentioned in passing by anybody in this batch.
- **Hands and means.** Nine coppers, no bread, water in her skin; bar she cannot hold at the heavy end, strap thinning; strip folded against her ribs refused forever on purpose; paper saying Foundation the first stage never opened; Clay Plate worn undescribed.
- **Yuan.** Hand. Readable without her, findable, held, sendable, answerable and asked at ch 423. Hand not spent and not refused in this batch; told her the price before she asked at ch 433 and is not arguing it again. No figure of what is left of him on any page. The Ledger returns 0.
- **In-world date.** Day 1,205, the twenty-second of the month beginning 1,184. Batch 0002 ran 1,153–1,205, fifty-three days. Batch 0003 opens day 1,206, the twenty-third.
- **Still open:** Hunter's request pending, line's own people still not knowing she exists · everything above the offices that vouched unread and unnamed · the fine hand and the man who can read it, both spent and neither given back · kiln-born in two heads and on no paper · what became of the eleven · register reconciled and unread · plate worn and undescribed · year carrying and mentioned zero times · MEMBER ABSENT carried, never raised · relicensing legs in order, none invented · claim answered, not reopened · Hou paid never met · Ning Wu's decade a line with no mark, none of the three on the page · strip under a stone at the mill · carter at the cart stand · turnstile man with his kept decision · reader of twenty-six · woman of twenty-two with nine months to a bad night · four hundred and seven names, one there by her mouth.

### Deviations from this prompt and from the outline, recorded where taken

1. **Ch 440 is titled The Question He Would Not Answer, not Nine Feet Off The Road.** The latter stood at ch 347 and titles must be unique in the book; renamed once, beat unmoved.
2. **The Hand is not spent on a man at a gate in this batch.** `outline/volume-08.md` §Yuan's track carded one spending here; this batch's own prompt §What the batch must not do forbids spending or refusing it, and the prompt governs the page. The spending is carried forward, not dropped — Batch 0003 owns the listing surfacing and Batch 0004 owns the refusal.
3. **Money closes at nine coppers, not eleven.** Eleven is the opening figure; bread west, water at seven miles and a night's floor are walked on the page in Batch 0001's method. The chapter wins.
4. **Ch 438's question is about Xie Changyin herself.** The prompt bars choosing Ning Yan's question for her and bars any question putting a third person in a room; asking about the woman in the room keeps both.
5. **Batch length sits under the 1,800 guide (21,673 / ~1,445) and above the 1,300 floor in every chapter.** Recorded honest rather than padded; every chapter is a finished scene with movement, and the midpoint, mouths, turnstile beat, Hunter page, Wei Ran card and irreversible step are all on the page. **The first draft's 20,914 / ~1,394 was inflated by the duplication now removed; the repaired figure is lower in the middle of the batch and higher at ch 445, because duplication was replaced with scene, not deleted.**

### Repair pass — Volume 8 Batch 0002, after the review of 14 Oct 2026

The review found five things. All five are cleared on the page. Nothing was restarted, no planned beat was moved, and the batch's day table, money table, irreversible step and chapter titles are untouched.

1. **Duplicated paragraphs — cleared.** Six whole-paragraph duplicates removed (ch 440, 441, 446, 447, 448, 449) plus the doubled midpoint at ch 445, where `## The Reading` and `## What She Read` narrated one reading twice and `## The Book Opens` re-narrated the naming. ch 445 now runs **The Reading → Four Hundred And Seven → The Discovery → The Road Out**, one telling of the reading and one of the roll. The freed space went into scene, not padding: **the young reader of twenty-six now objects to the question before it is asked, and is right**, and **the woman of twenty-two asks whether the hand is a bad one and is refused, and does not ask twice.** Both were already on the cards and neither changed the outcome.
2. **Authorial scaffolding — cleared, and the grep repaired.** The row claimed 0 for a pattern that required a numeral and so returned 0 by construction. Seven real hits are recast into ground: *a hundred days back on the road*, *the hedge a month back*, *the denial*, *the ninth*, *four days ago*, *every time a hand comes in*. **The bare chapter citations at ch 438 and ch 439 are the substance of this fix — a chapter number is a filing instruction, not a memory, and a character does not remember by chapter.**
3. **Prompt language in the prose — cleared.** *right about nine things out of ten* was lifted word for word from the writer's own prompt and stood at ch 447 and twice at ch 449. Xie Changyin now **keeps a written count of the one time in ten she is wrong**, and ch 449 says why she does not boast: a count you keep is not a boast, it is a tool. The characterisation is the same and it is now a woman with a ledger rather than a phrase with a woman in it.
4. **Length regression — resolved by the repair rather than by padding.** The batch was short against Batch 0001's 1,976 average because several chapters restated beats instead of dramatising them. De-duplicating alone would have made it shorter; the duplication was **replaced** with scene, and the batch now sits at 21,673 / ~1,445, every chapter above the 1,300 floor, ch 445 at 1,971 the only chapter over 1,700.
5. **Ch 449 closing on a state-file inventory — cleared.** The last line was a clause-for-clause copy of the Batch 0003 handoff paragraph. It is now prose: what eleven miles bought, what the roll holds after she is dead, the woman of twenty-two still not knowing, and the silence as the only term in the whole of it not yet spent.

**Also repaired in passing, found while fixing the above and not raised by the review:** a chronology break in ch 449, where the evening at the gate and the last lamp ran before the minute in the road that they follow — sections reordered, which is why the line at the gate had to be written twice; an internal contradiction in ch 448, where a stone carried two pebbles in one section and three in another, the third being herself — the three-pebble beat now runs once, in the morning, and the third is swept off; a run-on speech at ch 445 leaving one quote unclosed; four date slips at ch 446 that called the ninth *yesterday* and *the next day*; and two restatements of the same sentence at ch 440 and ch 443. **No plot was moved to fix any of this.**
