Recorded as deviation 2 below. **Day 1,291 is the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,274 and is the pinned Nascent Soul 9 day.** **1,291 − 1,152 = 139 days**, the interval the volume spends 139 days on. **1,340 − 1,256 = 84 days**, the length of his silence after the stop, and ch 478 says eighty-four.

### The money table, every figure on a page, and it adds up

**Enter day 1,263: five coppers in the fold stitched shut in the inside of a coat, and no bread since the twenty-eighth of a month two months back, which is day 1,211.**

| Day | Fold | What moved it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1,263–1,325 | **5, unchanged** | nothing bought, nothing given, nothing offered and nothing taken across fourteen chapters and the whole of the finest trade on nine days of road |
| **1,326** | **5 → 3** | the store five miles west of the mill, **one loaf at two coppers**, bought on the second day of a thirteen-mile walk, **because she wanted it** — a body that has not wanted bread since she was fourteen |
| 1,327–1,340 | **3, unchanged** | no spending; she ate about half the loaf on the second afternoon and the rest on the first |
| closes | **three coppers, and bread for a day in a piece of paper** | — |

**Four figures stood all the way through and are still standing at the close.** The rate behind the counter is two coppers a page and her hand has never closed on it; the four coppers for the fine hand were counted out on wood at ch 447 and swept back untaken and are not touched in this batch; the eight coppers remain a credit on Wei Ran's book and she says so out loud in a road at ch 468 and does not chase them; and the maximum rate offered at ch 473 — **four coppers for every page of any hand, no second rate and no third rate** — is untakeable on the same grounds and is never put on a table.

**The outline's §*Final chapter image* says *twenty-one coppers in the fold* and that figure belongs to Volume 7's closing position. The page does not support it and the chapter wins: ch 478 spends two coppers at a store five miles west of the mill and closes with three.** Recorded as deviation 1.

### What this batch established, and what it closed

- **CH 465 — THE LANE ASKING FOR NOTHING.** Four sheets a day on a bench at the end of the lane with a stone on the top one, because she can add two columns and take one off and a woman of fifty has had nobody who can do that for nineteen years. **The floor asks her about carts for the first time** — the last thing in the book that wants something from her. The woman of fifty's eleven days of silence break on the twentieth: she asks *did you put the dates in my column*, Ning Yan says **no**, the woman asks *then who does*, and Ning Yan gives her a silence. **Her third true answer that is not an answer in four days**, and the woman of fifty reads it and says *I am not going to ask you again, and that is a different thing from not asking now*. Four days of nothing; nobody thanks her for the half; **she works out that a sheet a day is a hand on a wheel and not a kindness**. On the twenty-fifth at the ninth hour she asks him for the whole of it, once, and **nothing comes back for the length of a night**, and she understands for the first time that a man who has nothing to spend is not a man who has chosen not to spend one.
- **CH 466 — ONE THOUSAND ONE HUNDRED AND NINE.** At the fourth hour on the twenty-sixth, in the ordinary voice, in the present tense, once: **one thousand one hundred and nine days and six hours, the whole of it, from the eighth of the month it started in to the twenty-sixth of this one** — and **twenty-one days and four hours, which is what he put where a hand would find it, three times on this ground, and he told her about one of the three.** Then one sentence: **you did not ask me.** She asks him to say it again and he says it again identically, which tells her the figure was not worked out the night before; it has been sitting there for seven years. She writes it on a board, then scrapes it off, because **a column is a request**. **He never once calls the omission a kindness and she works out that a man who names a thing has decided something, and he has never decided anything, and a thing that is not decided is still running.**
- **CH 467 — SEVENTEEN HOURS OUT OF EVERY HUNDRED.** Four days of arithmetic on the boards. **Twenty-six thousand six hundred and twenty-two hours. About six thousand five hundred days in an eighteen-year life. Seventeen out of every hundred. A little over three years and eleven days out of the four years she has been a person a place can put its hand on — and not one day of it she can point at, because it did not come out of a day.** **One in fifty-three is everything she caught in seven weeks of counting.** The hole: **one thousand and eighty-eight days and two hours**, and the finding of four days — **it is not for the work. The twenty-one days are the times he did a thing. The rest is the price of a person being findable from outside, once, by anybody.** She does not find out who set the price, will not go through the door in it, and does not put it anywhere. Then the want: **she has the two dates of the second column — the eighth of a month seven years back and the twenty-fourth of a month — and there is no book on nine days of road with a column for the pair.**
- **CH 468 — THE BOARD BEHIND THE COUNTER.** **Seventy-five days since she held a silence in daylight, and nothing has stopped, and nobody has asked her one single question** — which is not a silence she keeps. The rate board read a fourth time; her own empty hand flat on the counter wood for two seconds. **Wei Ran at the gate: four journeys east since the second, two back, none of the four came back with anything in it, said as a trade and not a favour; eight coppers still on the book and never chased; and then she says *Ning Yan* out loud in a road with four men unloading within nine feet and a boy on a cart within twenty.** **That is the whole mechanism of the ground: one person says a name in a road and every one of them hears it again.** The young reader of twenty-six, **four sentences, no scene**: *I am not going to ask you to read anything. I said a thing in that room about you and the room did it anyway, and I have had four months to work out what it cost me to be right, and the answer is nothing.* Xie Changyin has not been told anything and does not ask: **the offer is final after the twelfth, she is going up the road for the season, and a woman of thirty-one will make it.**
- **CH 469 — A SHEET UNDER A LAMP.** A fine hand, tied, dated on a slip of paper, four days on a bench. Four people cannot read it and **Ning Yan says nothing for four days and does not say why.** Then the man with the brush asks, in the middle of a room, and she says no, and instead tells the room the thing she has never told anybody: **you cannot read it because there is one of me and I have been able to read it for eight years and did not learn it anywhere and was never taught it.** **The reason she knows what a fine hand looks like is that she has spent two years refusing to have one read to her, and you cannot refuse a thing you have not recognised.** What she does not say: that there is a sheet about *her*, and that her no is the reason a woman of sixty-one walked eleven miles. Xie Changyin promises, in the ordinary voice, that the room will not ask her again. It goes east on the twenty-second on a string. **Her want at the end: she wanted to read it more than anything in nine months and she has not got a way to read one without being entered by it.**
- **CH 470 — THE CEILING OF A REALM.** Four days of the ring coming up a finger's width and stopping, and **on the fourth she stops doing it on purpose**, because for eighty-four days nobody is paying for anything and she will not beg for a second pair of hands. Xie Changyin sets the date: **the twelfth, the terms final, and then she goes up the road for the season.** On the eighteenth at the fourth hour, in the long room with the shutters up and about thirty people in it, **the ninth stage arrives without her asking and cannot be stopped** — she says *no* out loud in the ordinary voice and it does not take, **which is ch 434's cost paying off in the worst place it could**. [Ning Yan, past the line, servant-born, under no ceiling any county holds. Nascent Soul, stage nine, is held.] Then nothing, which is what frightens her. She gets out into the road at the gate and lies in the dirt and the man at the gate does not come over and does not call after her. **Cost, named once:** the small open place low behind her fourth rib, quiet since the twenty-first of a month four months back, **opens again and does not close, and there is nothing above her to close it with.** **There is nothing above her: every technique runs out at nine, every resource she can hold runs out at nine, and the only door in the world is a piece of paper against her ribs that has never been opened.** She does not open it, and the reason is not fear — **it is that she has just worked out, in a room with a lamp in it, exactly what it is to have a document read aloud to you.**
- **CH 471 — THE VERSION WHERE HE IS THERE.** A fine hand on a bench with a girl of twenty-two on it and a month of her life on it. Ning Yan says no twice and **wants to use him.** Then, out loud, in the present tense, with four people in the room, **the Hand is refused**, and the reason is given for the first time: **a hand only works where somebody's hand already is; if he puts it in front of her, then the next one goes in front of a man's hand, and a man at a gate takes a bar off a wall because a painted board said so; and he has never once been able to do the version where he is there to see what it does.** **That is not ch 419's reason and she says so out loud: that one was about her and this one is not about her at all, and it does not protect anybody.** And it **costs her nothing** — the first refusal in seven years that comes out clean, which she finds is worse than being taken from. The cart goes east on the twenty-eight with the girl's whole life on it.
- **CH 472 — WHAT A FORM IS.** Worked out on a cart sheet at the end of a lane: **a form is a page with a place on it for a person to put themselves**, and **the sheet is read to you because you cannot read it yourself** — the paper is in the fine hand, a paper can be folded into four and taken home or read out of a room by somebody paid two coppers a page, **and the reason it is read to you is not fairness. It is read to you so that there is no copy.** Recruitment, said by her and not by anybody else: **a reading performed on a person, by somebody qualified to read it, in a room, on the condition that the person confirms or denies.** The maximum: **one rate, four coppers for every page, no second and no third, and the board behind the counter is wrong and she is not going to have it repainted.** Xie Changyin dates the reading for the second at the fourth hour with the man who keeps the book in the room.
- **CH 473 — THE SHEET READ ALOUD TO HER.** The first of a month, at the fourth hour, with the shutters up. Five terms, all fair, the sixth line the shortest: **this document has been read to you, do you agree to it.** She asks for the column of what it costs, **which is the one thing she wanted from this trade**, and gets it in Xie Changyin's own hand, unvouched and unread aloud, because **there is no hand in the world that can check a figure a woman has put down in her own hand** — which is exactly what happened to Ning Yan at a mill when she was fifteen or sixteen. Four lines: four fingers, half an arm, the turn, the order — **and a fifth line that is not a cost: *nine of them are in that room and I do not know one of their names*.** She agrees to answer on the second. **Xie Changyin tells her, unasked, that her heart is going at about nine in the morning.**
- **CH 474 — HER NAME IN THE BEST BOOK.** **THE BATCH'S IRREVERSIBLE STEP.** She reads the sheet **to them first**, out loud, in four seconds, and then tells the room the reason she can read a hand they cannot — because her refusal in a room four months back is why a trade of eleven hundred years is sending a sheet it cannot read east on a cart — and then says **no**, with no reason in front of it. Xie Changyin writes it and **reads it back to her in the ordinary voice before it goes in**, which is the ceremony turned the right way round once. **Ning Yan makes her take back the one line that is hers** and the woman refuses: a thing not put in is not a refusal, it is a person who did not turn up. **So she reads her own refusal back to them**, and a man of sixty-one writes one line and the date in the ordinary hand, and **there is no hand in that building that takes it out.** It is the fourth entry of her life and the first one she chose.
- **CH 475 — WHAT A QUIET GROUND LOOKS LIKE.** Four days, in order, and it does not happen on one day. **The bench is empty on the seventh.** The woman of fifty says the plain reason: **there is nothing coming down the lane for me to check** — a cart brings a sheet, a cart brings a thing, and what is on the carts is what the building has read. **She bought her own anonymity with her own usefulness**, said out loud on the bank and again later under a hedge. The man of sixty-one: **the folder will go east on the first with nothing on it, and there is nowhere in the book to put that, and he has been looking since the fifth of a month.** The man behind the counter asks her whether there is something and she says there is not. **The young reader of twenty-six puts it in one word — *two* — and says the thing nobody else will say: that what has stopped is not the reading, it is a woman of fifty putting a number on a bench for six days and then not doing it on the seventh.** **And under a hedge in the rain she admits the want she has never admitted: she wants to be the sort of person a ground reaches for, without it going into a book, and no arrangement on nine days of road has ever offered it.**
- **CH 476 — THE PRICE OF NOT BEING FOUND.** No one to ask — the Hunter's two pages are spent and she is not going to go and find him. Five things have ever come for her in eight years and **all five came out of a document**; there is no road into her life except paper, and that is eight years old and she is the only person who could have found it. **A person who is not read is harder to find**, worked out at a gate four feet off a post: the hand on a gate reads a painted board because the board is where its hands are, and take the paper away and the hand does not stop. The third finding is the one she is not ready for: **she is at the top of a realm at eighteen, with no wages, no season, no pay she can take and no institution that has heard of her — and it is the best thing that has happened to her in two years, and she chose it, and she cannot say so out loud to anybody including him.** She writes the whole list on a board and one line at the bottom, well away from the rest.
- **CH 477 — A DESCRIPTION, AND IT IS ACCURATE.** **MOUTH SIX OF SIX.** Xie Changyin walks two miles down the lane at the sixth hour, sixty-one, carrying nothing. Her mouth, in pieces: *you take a sheet four people cannot take and make it into numbers, and you say the numbers out loud to whoever is in the room, and you do it for nothing, and you have been paid for it once in four coppers and your hand stopped. That is the trade.* **The trap named by the woman selling it: what she has been trying to hire her for is what she has been doing for nothing since she was sixteen, and the only thing there is to sell anybody is that somebody will be standing in the room when she does it.** **The one wrong sentence, said once, and refused once, and not argued:** *a person on a roll is safer than a person off it.* **The one thing Ning Yan wins:** the room will not ask her again, the tied sheet goes east unopened, and **a girl of twenty-two goes on not knowing what a hand said about her** — and Xie Changyin says plainly that she will not thank her and will not apologise, because both would be deciding how she feels about a thing done on purpose. Then she gives the shelf, the room and the date on the string for the two sheets the woman nine years ago wrote out and did not sign, **and calls it an ordinary consequence and not a favour.** Ning Yan writes the address on a board, scrapes it off, and does it anyway.
- **CH 478 — FORTY-ONE LINES ON A RAIL.** Thirteen miles west in three days — two of lane and eleven of made road, the whole distance on the page. **One loaf at the store five miles west of the mill, two coppers, five down to three, because she wanted it.** The roof cord holds; the ring is fed; the door does not shut properly and she does not fix it. **She does not say his name for eleven days, which has never happened.** At the sixth hour on the seventh she reads all forty-one lines on the rail down twice and gets them the same twice and can take none of them. **Then she notices she has been standing there eleven minutes and nothing has happened: not the boy, not the man behind the counter, not the man with the brush.** Four people on that floor asked her a question a month and wrote the answer down; a woman of fifty asked her about carts every day for two months. **Nothing on nine days of road wants anything from her at all.** Three coppers, bread for a day, eighteen years old, at the top of a realm, nothing above her, and a piece of paper against her ribs she has not opened. She says his name once into a shed with boards on the floor. **He has had nothing to spend for eighty-four days and there is nothing left to answer with.** **She waits anyway.**

### The irreversible step, and what it cost

**Ch 474. She refuses the Order in a room, in daylight, at the fourth hour, with the shutters up, on the ordinary condition, and her name goes into the best book on nine days of road with the third of a month against it, read back to her before it went in.**

It cannot be undone. It is the fourth entry of her life and the first one she asked for, and there is no hand in that building or on that ground that takes a line out of that book. She gave up the one place on nine days of road that would have paid her for the only thing she is good at — and **she could not have taken the four coppers a page anyway, which is the whole of why it was a seduction rather than a job.**

**The cost is not punishment. The Order does not punish anybody and Xie Changyin said so three times before the batch opened and once on the day. The cost is that the Order stops reading her, and the ground goes quiet four days later, and at the last page nothing on nine days of road wants anything from her at all.** She bought her own anonymity with her own usefulness. **It is the best thing that has happened to her in two years, and she chose it, and she cannot say so out loud to anybody including him.**

### Volume 8 Batch 0004 running ledger — at the last page, day 1,340

- **Ning Yan — NASCENT SOUL 9**, taken on the pinned day **1,291, the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,274**, at the fourth hour, in a long room with the shutters up, **without her asking and without her being able to stop it.** Bracket 2 of Volume 8's two in fifty-eight. **Bracket 2 of 2. The budget is spent.**
- **Body.** Four fingers and a thumb on the right hand. Right arm open half the time. Eight of eleven holds. Short breath four counts everywhere including inside a loaded reach. Aperture cracked through and permanent. **The small open place low behind her fourth rib, quiet since the twenty-first of a month four months back, came open on the eighteenth and is not going to close.** Not hungry, and she has worked out that this is not a gift. Eighteen years old.
- **Eight costs stand, none re-derived and none broken:** the furnace will not light itself · the hand cannot write a false line · a thing reached cannot be put back · she cannot be given anything · she cannot be paid · the reach answers a question and only a question · she cannot call a halt — herself, once a thing she is in has started · **and there is nothing above her, and the way out is a document, and she has not opened hers.**
- **What she gave up on purpose and did not get back.** What the fine hand says about her. Spent at ch 417. **Nothing in ch 465–478 reopens it, softens it, queries it or names it, and at ch 474 she tells a room full of strangers that she is the only person on nine days of road who can read the hand they cannot and never says a word about the sheet with her name at the head of it.** At ch 470 she wants to open the document against her ribs more than she has wanted anything and does not.
- **Hands and means.** **Three coppers in the fold, stitched shut, and bread for a day.** Water in her skin and the trough at seven miles, eleven miles from the shed she is sleeping in. Bar strap rebound with her own coat lining. Piece of paper against her ribs, never opened. Clay Plate worn, undescribed. The relicensed licence is never opened and the word is never used in this batch or anywhere in Volume 8.
- **Yuan.** Hand. Readable without her, findable, held, sendable, answerable and asked. **Three spends in Batch 0003 and none in this one. The fourth was put down at ch 462 and has not been spent.** **The Hand refused once out loud, in a room, at ch 471, with the reason given for the first time, and it cost her nothing.** Two utterances of his own in the whole batch are the bill, at ch 466, and everything else he says is at ch 471. **No figure of what is left of him is stated on any page in ch 465–478, before the bill or after it.** The Ledger returns 0 and the word does not appear.
- **In-world date.** **Day 1,340, the seventh of the month beginning 1,334.** Batch 0004 ran 1,263–1,340, seventy-eight days. Volume 8 runs 1,091–1,340, 250 days.
- **Where she is at the last page.** **A shed with boards on the floor four miles east of a mill whose wheel has not turned since before she came**, with a bar against the wall, a ring with clay in it, a roof cord, a door that does not shut properly, and a bar-strap she bound out of her own coat lining. Thirteen miles east of her is a lane with a floor on it and a crossing a mile further. Eleven miles east of that is a gate with a chain and a lime-washed building with a lamp in every window. Nine days of road west is a mill, a counting house, a copying floor, a third floor, a room with a lamp, and a lifting floor with a rail outside it and forty-one lines on a board.
- **Still open:** the Hunter's request pending, both of his Volume 8 pages spent at 428 and 440 · everything above the offices that vouched unread and unnamed · the fine hand and the man who can read it, both spent and neither given back · *kiln-born* in two heads and on no paper · what became of the eleven · the register still *reconciled* and unread · the plate still worn and undescribed · **the year carrying and mentioned zero times in fourteen chapters** · MEMBER ABSENT carried, never raised · the relicensing legs in order, none invented · the claim answered, not reopened · Hou paid and never met · Ning Wu's decade a line with no mark · the strip under a stone at the mill · the turnstile man with his kept decision · **the young reader of twenty-six, told nothing and owed nothing, who has now said twice in two chapters that being right cost him nothing** · **a woman of twenty-two with a bad night in nine months and a sheet on a cart east of the turn that nobody will read** · **a tie on the string, a date, and the address of a shelf and a room and two sheets written by a woman nine years ago who did not sign them, given and not asked for** · two yeses without meaning · **a chair at a crossing under a cloth with a light in the hut behind it** · **and thirteen lines in a left column, two of them the layer's, which nobody on that ground can explain and which the layer will not be told about.**

### Untouched, unraised, still true at ch 478

Feng 0. Ning Wu 0. Wen 0. Shen Xiu 0. Qiu, Mei Jiu, Du Hong, the sponsors 0. **The year mentioned 0 times.** No office named; **no department, floor, rank list, committee, house or title beyond recruited**; the Order's building, its book, its rate and its rule are the only things about it that are ever on the page and **bare capital `Order` returns 3 across the batch, all of them referring back to a name given once at ch 425.** The Order's budget never stated; its reason never explained. **The surface answer at ch 267 not said, hinted, gestured or paraphrased; the Order never explains why a bounded world needs readers.** **The tenth part not given and nothing proves the crews wrong.** **No harem and nothing a romance; her mother's name is never said.** Register never read. **The plate is named once in Volume 8, at ch 441 in Batch 0002, and is not described in any chapter.** **The relicensed licence never opened. The word `licence` / `license` returns 0 across this batch and 3 across Volume 8 — at ch 425 and ch 449, both in Xie Changyin's mouth, both describing the Order's own paper, which the outline pins as the trade's term. Neither of the three is the relicensed licence.** Ashwood, kiln-born, MEMBER ABSENT, Anchor, Sisterhood, Scribe, bargain, Lung, appointment, clearance, countersignature, tribulation, drift, hiring, audit, tending, ration, confinement, kill, murder — **all 0 across this batch.** **No stage-day, relicensing leg, clearance, seal or countersignature shown, and no card says one was or was not required.** **No death in this batch.** Nothing of the ending previewed: no death named, the Anchor not placed, the Sisterhood not touched, the bargain not on the table, Feng not on the page. **Xie Changyin does not learn the stop, the death, the two figures, the bill, or what the fine hand says about Ning Yan; she is told about none of it and asks about none of it.** Wei Ran appears once, at the gate, in a road, as a trade. **The Hunter does not appear and both of his pages in Volume 8 remain spent (428, 440).**

### The sixteen continuity facts, walked one at a time, against the chapters

1. **The relicensing legs stay in order** — clearance → seal → countersignature. **Volume 8's allowance for all three is 0 and it is 0.** `clearance` and `countersignature` return zero across ch 421–478. The relicensed licence **stays never opened**, and Volume 8 raises what it is worth three times (ch 470, ch 476 and ch 478) **without ever reading it**.
2. **MEMBER ABSENT stands three times, permanent, carried and never raised.** Zero across the volume.
3. **The register still reads *reconciled*, and is not read.** Zero across the volume.
4. **The Clay Plate is still worn against her ribs and has not been described.** Zero across the volume. At ch 476 she walks four miles with a leaded thing strapped flat against her ribs and does not name or describe it.
5. **What became of the eleven is not raised; the four months erased are never named; the ch 128 silence is unbroken; the ch 150 refusal is not softened; no second Lung; no second appointment; Feng never on the page.** All zero.
6. **His unentered state and the date of the morning he died are gone, and nothing gives either back.** Both still gone; neither is named in this batch.
7. **The claim is answered and not reopened; Hou Sanliang is paid, never met, off the page; Ning Bo's account discharged and Ning Wu's decade a bare line; neither on the page.** All true.
8. **No harem, nothing a romance; her mother's name never said; Yuan never says what he was before he died.** All true.
9. **Wei Ran — twenty, a trade, a team, a rate and an opinion, at most two cards in Volume 8.** **Two cards: ch 441 and ch 468, and ch 468 is the whole of the second** — five sentences, no scene, no gift, no romance, and she is a trade.
10. **No office above the Standing Office of the Second Account is named; nothing above the copying floor or the second office; the Order is named once as an institution.** The Order's name stands once in the volume, at ch 425. **Bare capital `Order` returns 3 in this batch and 7 across the volume, every one referring back to that single naming.**
11. **The Hunter's request stays pending. A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent. The line's own people stop not knowing she exists in Batch 0004, and it is not the Hunter's doing.** **It is Wei Ran's: a woman of twenty says her name out loud in a road at the gate with four men unloading within nine feet, and Ning Yan's own reading at the sixth hour over eleven weeks, and both of those are on the page at ch 468 and ch 465.** The Hunter is absent.
12. ***Kiln-born* stays in two heads and on no paper.** Zero.
13. **The Ledger.** Zero across ch 465–478 and zero across ch 421–478, measured on a word boundary.
14. **The year of non-forgiveness is still running, and the subject is not mentioned once in fifty-eight chapters.** **Zero across ch 421–478, measured.** The bill at ch 466 is a bill: she does not thank him, she does not argue it, she does not put it on a board, and no card says otherwise.
15. **The channel: no figure of what is left of him stated on any page, before the bill or after it.** **True. The bill is a figure of a total and not a figure of him, and ch 466 and ch 467 say so by never using it as one.** The last figure in the manuscript before this volume is *about six weeks* at ch 236.
16. **His readability: readable from outside the bound without her and therefore findable; addressed as a holder and therefore sendable; answerable since ch 401, and Volume 8 is when somebody asks.** **All three are on the page in this batch and they are what the bill is for:** ch 467's finding that the standing draw is the price of being findable is the first time the cost of the three is stated as a number, and it is stated by her, out of a figure he gave her, and **she does not learn who set the price and says so on the page.**
17. **What Volume 8 changed:** Nascent Soul 7 → 9, the ceiling of a realm · she cannot call a halt · there is nothing above her and the door is a document · the Order is named and refused and stops reading her · her ch 417 refusal was read correctly by strangers and is the reason the Order came · he spent Hand on three people who are not her · **he gave her the bill, in a number, once** · she made him stop and he has had nothing to spend for eighty-four days · the fourth frame scene is on the page at ch 458 · one man east of the turn does not come back from a listing and it is his act and she finds out four days late · and at the last page **nothing on nine days of road wants anything from her at all.**

### Volume 8 as a whole, closed, re-measured

**`chapters/volume-08/` holds ch 421–478, fifty-eight chapters, 107,669 words of body prose**, by batch **0001 (ch 421–434) 28,334 · 0002 (ch 435–449) 21,869 · 0003 (ch 450–464) 28,341 · 0004 (ch 465–478) 29,125**, average ~1,856, counting the `#` title line as excluded and every `##` section heading as prose. **On the other convention, title and section headings both excluded, the same file set is 105,846 — 0001 27,666 · 0002 21,681 · 0003 27,879 · 0004 28,620. Both figures are recorded here because this run has published both at different times, and a reader of either one alone will think the other is wrong. The Batch 0004 figure moved from 29,186 to 29,125 in the batch review repair and the volume figure from 107,730 to 107,669 with it; the first three are untouched by that repair.** **THE VOLUME 8 CLOSE HAS SETTLED THE CONVENTION, AND IT IS METHOD B — the `#` title line and every `##` section heading excluded. ON THAT METHOD THE VOLUME IS 105,846 WORDS OF BODY PROSE OVER FIFTY-EIGHT CHAPTERS, AVERAGE ~1,825, BY BATCH 27,666 · 21,681 · 27,879 · 28,620.** The method A figures in this paragraph stand as they were written and are correct on their own convention; **where this block and §*Volume 8 close — the volume as a whole* disagree, the close block is right.** **478 `#` headings manuscript-wide, zero collisions, longest 39 characters.** Day 1,091 to day 1,340, 250 days. **Two bracketed layer lines in fifty-eight, at ch 434 and ch 470, in the far-side house form character for character with only the stage number changed — the budget is spent and Volume 9 opens on no bracket in Volume 8's tail.**

**Twenty-nine chapter files in Volume 8 have no trailing newline** — Batch 0001's fourteen, Batch 0003's fifteen, and none in Batch 0002 or Batch 0004. **The Volume 7 close fixed 250 such files as an operational repair; Volume 8 has reintroduced 29.** Not a word-count error, and the per-file figures above are unaffected, but it is how a chapter that ends without a line break collides with the next file in a concatenated read. **Recorded for the Volume 8 close, not fixed here.**

**Measured across all fifty-eight chapters of Volume 8, not only across this batch, because the next phase needs the volume figure and not the batch figure.** Authorial scaffolding **0** · bracketed layer lines **2** · named weekdays **0**, named calendar months **0**, named weeks **0** (fifteen `May` hits on a case-insensitive grep are **all the modal *may*** — *you may ask me*, *she may ask*, *May I ask you something*, *you may read every book* — and none of them is a month) · the retired seven phrases **0** · blockquotes, code fences, tables and horizontal rules **0 / 0 / 0 / 0** · all-caps standalone lines **0** · `reconciled` **0** · `kiln-born` **0** · `MEMBER ABSENT` **0** · `Ledger` **0** · `tribulation` **0** · `Feng` **0** · `Ning Wu` **0** · `Anchor` **0** · `Sisterhood` **0** · `bargain` **0** · `Lung` **0** · `appointment` **0** · `clearance` **0** · `countersignature` **0**. **The scaffolding zero is re-measured after the batch review repair and holds: the bare citation `Volume 4` at ch 470, which is the only instance the widened pattern catches and which the pattern as printed in the budget row below cannot match, is recast to ground.**

**Three figures stand across the volume that do not stand in this batch, all of them in ch 421–464, none of them in the scope of this phase, and all three recorded here so that the next phase inherits the truth rather than a clean zero:**

1. **`licence` — 3, at ch 425 and ch 449**, both in Xie Changyin's mouth and both describing the Order's own paper, which `outline/volume-08.md` pins as the trade's term. **The relicensed licence against her ribs is never called anything.**
2. **`plate` — 1, at ch 441** in Batch 0002: *the Clay Plate rested worn against her ribs and she did not describe it even to herself.* **It is named once and never described in any chapter of the volume.** Batch 0003's own budget row reads *Plate never described* and does not record the naming, and this is the third time in this run that a state file has published a clean count on a token that stands on the page.
3. **Italic asterisks — 16, in ch 426, ch 433 and ch 448**, all in Batches 0001 and 0002. Batch 0004 carries **0 / 0** on bold and italic.

**None of these three was touched, because this phase was given a prompt that opens *Do not repair anything in ch 1–464* and closed on the same instruction. They are recorded here as findings for whichever phase owns a Volume 8 close audit, and that phase should walk this section rather than trust the per-batch rows.**

### Construction budget — Volume 8 Batch 0004 (ch 465–478), measured by grep and not promised

Method: fourteen files in `chapters/volume-08/`, body prose (the `#` title line and every `##` section heading excluded), whole words, case-insensitive unless noted, **counted before it is claimed and the true number written here. Every figure below was measured on the files as they stand after the final edit.**

### Deviations from this prompt and from the outline, recorded where taken

1. **The final image's money is three coppers and not twenty-one.** `outline/volume-08.md` §*Final chapter image* closes on *twenty-one coppers in the fold*, which is Volume 7's figure and which the page has not supported since ch 434. **Ch 478 spends two coppers on one loaf at the store five miles west of the mill and closes with three, and says so twice.** The chapter wins and the outline's figure is recorded as superseded. The spend is also the batch's smallest human beat and it is the one thing in fourteen chapters she buys because she wants it.
2. **The batch closes on day 1,340, which is the seventh of the month beginning 1,334.** The drafting prompt's last table row says *the seventh of the month beginning 1,304*, which is a month out; `outline/volume-08.md` says only *the seventh of a month* and `state/continuity.md` line 2423 had it right already. **Neither the chapter nor the outline asserts a month-start and the prose says *the seventh of a month*, so nothing on the page is wrong.** The prompt's row is recorded as superseded.
3. **Nascent Soul 9 arrives without her asking and without her being able to stop it, and there is no bracket anywhere else in the batch.** The outline's stage row gives the cost as *there is nothing above her* and the escalation table gives the reason for the 139-day interval as *the board is full, she cannot be given anything, the furnace will not light itself, and from ch 462 nobody is paying for a second pair of hands*. **All four of those reasons are on the page at ch 470 and she works them out and declines to beg, and then the stage arrives anyway.** That is recorded as a deliberate reading rather than a departure: a stage that arrives by itself is the last thing a person who has been read by three institutions for seven years can be handed, and the batch's own argument is that she does not want it. **It also pays off ch 434's cost in the worst place it can — she says no out loud and the no does not take — which is the pinned cost used once and not re-explained.**
4. **The Hand is refused at ch 471 in a room where four people are standing, and only she can hear it.** The outline pins *refused, out loud, in a room*. Four people heard a woman of twenty-two refuse a man with a brush and were correct about what they heard. The reason is *a hand only works where somebody's hand already is, and I cannot do the version where I am there*, which is **not** ch 419's reason, and she says so out loud in one sentence so that the difference is on the page rather than in a card. **The refusal costs her nothing**, which no refusal in the manuscript has ever done, and she finds that worse than being taken from.
5. **The one thing she wins at ch 477 is a sheet going east unopened, and it is not a favour.** The outline says *the one thing she wins — which is stated in the outline and is not a favour and is not an apology* and does not name it. This batch names it: **the room will not ask her again, and a girl of twenty-two goes on not knowing what a hand said about her.** Xie Changyin says in her own mouth that she will not thank her and will not apologise, and that both would be deciding how Ning Yan feels about a thing done on purpose in the best interests of a building. **She also gives the shelf, the room and the date on the string for the two sheets a woman wrote nine years ago and did not sign, and calls that an ordinary consequence and not a favour** — Ning Yan writes it on a board, scrapes it off, and writes it again anyway.
6. **The woman of twenty-two asks Ning Yan directly whether the hand is bad, in a doorway, on the second, and is refused in a sentence that has no reason in it.** The outline has her bad night still nine months out at the close of the volume and does not spend it. **It is not spent. She asks once, in a doorway, and is told the truth and told that the truth is not free.**
7. **The man of sixty-one says the strip is finished and the young reader of twenty-six says the word for what has stopped.** The batch card says *the man behind the counter goes back to asking her about nothing* and that is on the page. **The card does not say who names the mechanism, and two people do: the man of sixty-one, who has looked for a place to put it since the fifth of a month and found none, and the young reader of twenty-six, who says *two* and then explains that what has stopped is not the reading but a woman of fifty putting a number on a bench for six days and then not doing it on the seventh.** Both are correct people saying an unwelcome thing, which is the volume's temperature and not a new villain.
8. **Wei Ran's Batch 0004 card is spent at ch 468 and it is six sentences long.** The outline gives her a rate per person and a rate per load and a trade and nothing else. **She says the journey count out loud as a trade, she refuses to chase eight coppers, and then she says Ning Yan's name in a road with four men unloading within nine feet of her.** That last clause is the batch's rendering of the outline's *the line's own people stop not knowing she exists in Batch 0004, and it is not the Hunter's doing*, and it is the cheapest and most complete mechanism in the whole book: one person says a name at the wrong end of a cart.
9. **Two of Xie Changyin's ordinary lines are about a day she does not name.** She says the offer is final after the twelfth and that a woman of thirty-one will make it, and she says she is going up the road for the season and has stopped arranging her season around Ning Yan. **That is the one piece of agency the volume gives the institution and it is the reason ch 477's mouth lands**: the woman who wants Ning Yan has a life of her own that continues whether or not Ning Yan comes.
10. **The batch carries no chapter-level scene at the crossing, no man dying, and no new person on the ground east of the turn.** Batch 0003's shapes — the gate man reading a board, the sill, the ditch, the man on the bed, the chair under the cloth — are carried, not re-run. **The one figure carried forward and still unaccounted for is the chair, which has a cloth over it and a light in the hut behind it on the eleventh, and she does not go back.**

### Self-review pass — Volume 8 Batch 0004, run by the drafting phase before the phase closed

`AGENTS.md`'s quality gate asks for a reviewer. **The `novel-reviewer` subagent was invoked on the finished batch and failed again on this tier**, with the same error Batch 0003 recorded, so the review was run in the phase against its own chapters, in writing, and the findings are recorded here rather than claimed as a pass by somebody else. **Six findings were recorded while drafting and all six were fixed on the page before the phase closed**, a second sweep after the state files were drafted found two more, and **a third sweep — run across all fifty-eight chapters of the volume rather than across this batch, because the next phase needs the volume figure — found three figures that stand in ch 421–464 and that this phase was given a prompt forbidding it to repair. Those three are recorded above under *Volume 8 as a whole, closed, re-measured* and are findings for whichever phase owns a Volume 8 close audit.**

1. **A named weekday on the page at ch 477** — *it is Tuesday of my life*, inside a beat that then had both characters decline to hear it. **Cut.** The construction was scaffolding wearing a day of the week's clothes, and a card is not a place to smuggle one.
2. **Thirty instances of authorial scaffolding at first measure** — `seven volumes` twenty-three times, `two volumes` three, `this book` four, and bare `ch 417` and `ch 425`. **All thirty recast.** The two chapter citations were the serious ones and they are the failure mode a volume-count pattern does not catch by itself.
3. **Five banned canon tokens at first measure** — `licence` ×4, `bargain` ×2, `plate` ×1 — each sitting on a load-bearing sentence. **All five recast.** The relicensed licence against her ribs is now *a piece of paper* throughout, the Order's first term is *a paper that says a paper*, and ch 476's four-mile walk is *a leaded thing strapped flat against her ribs*.
4. **Thirteen instances of retired calendar vocabulary** — *about four seconds* ×7, *eleven weeks* ×3, *fortnight* ×3. **All thirteen recast** into counts of breath, counts of seconds or month-dates.
5. **`whole of` at 42 across the batch** — nearly three times Batch 0003's 17 and four times Batch 0002's. **Twenty-six cut, sixteen left**, reproducing the last two batches. The ones left in Xie Changyin's mouth are a character's habit.
6. **A paragraph of 132 words in ch 474** and three others over 100. **All split.** No paragraph in the batch is over 95.
7. **The second sweep, after the state files were drafted: ch 465 stated the stage interval as a hundred and thirty-nine days on day 1,263**, which is the *completed* interval and cannot be known on that day. **Corrected to a hundred and eleven days of not getting stronger**, which is the elapsed figure on the pinned day and is checkable.
8. **The second sweep: ch 478 twice said *ninety days* for the length of his silence**, against the outline's pinned eighty-four (1,340 − 1,256). **Corrected to eighty-four in both places.** The prompt names eighty-four twice and a drafter that writes ninety out of round numbers has the same disease as a state file that publishes a clean count it has not measured.
9. **The second sweep: two chapter endings landed soft** — ch 465 on *the lane asked her for nothing at all* and ch 474 on the man of sixty-one shutting the book. **Both extended with the beat that was missing:** ch 465 now closes on the one thing she cannot do, which is ask him a second question, so that ch 466's unasked-for answer arrives as a live problem; and ch 474 now closes on the four entries of her life and the fact that she is the only person who chose this one.

**Checked by that pass, with the figure:** every chapter ends on a live beat and none on a neutral action · **zero authorial scaffolding, counted on the widened pattern and on the files as they stand** · zero named weekdays, named months and named weeks · the seven retired phrases zero as phrases · zero bold, zero italic, zero blockquote, zero code fence, zero table, zero horizontal rule, zero all-caps standalone line · **zero straight quotes and zero straight apostrophes in all fourteen files and every speech closed in all fourteen** · **one bracketed layer line in fourteen chapters and it is at ch 470** · zero of every banned canon token on word boundaries, including `licence`, `plate`, `bargain`, `kill` and `murder` · **478 headings manuscript-wide with zero collisions and a longest of 39 characters** · the day table re-derives under `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` with no gap and no overlap, the pinned 1,291 holds, and **1,340 − 1,256 = 84** · **every sentence Yuan speaks is in the present tense and there is no marked present frame anywhere in the batch, because Batch 0003 owns the only one in the volume** · and every person on the page is correct, including the man behind the counter, who asks whether there is something and is told there is not, and the young reader of twenty-six, who has now said twice that being right has cost him nothing.

### Volume 8 Batch 0004 — external review, and the repair pass that answered it

**The review of `volume-08-batch-0004` returned nine findings: three must-fix, four should-fix and two on process. All nine are closed. No chapter was restarted. No beat, date anchor, coin figure, character mouth, spend, stop, bracket or stage moved, and nothing in the planned plot changed. Thirty-one sentences were touched across six of the fourteen files, and the batch is 65 words shorter than the drafting phase left it.**

**The review's verdict, recorded because the prose is not what changed: the batch passes every mechanical gate.** Fourteen chapters, every speech closed, zero straight marks, zero bold, zero italic, zero blockquote, code fence, table or horizontal rule, exactly one bracketed system line and it is at ch 470, zero banned canon tokens, longest paragraph 95, 478 headings with zero collisions, day arithmetic re-deriving on all four pinned anchors, the irreversible step real and its price not punishment but loss of being wanted.

#### The three that had to be fixed

1. **ch 478 contradicted itself on how many times she has said his name, and the volume's last scene is built on the number.** One line said eleven answered and about thirty unanswered in seven years; the closing beat said eleven in seven years, four answered, this the twelfth — forty-one occasions against twelve. **The count now stands once and only once, at the first statement: eleven occasions in seven years, four of them answered, every one of the eleven inside a dozen days of a spend. The closing beat is now *It is the twelfth time in seven years, and the first one that has not come inside a dozen days of something happening to him*, and it no longer restates the tally it is standing on.**
2. **ch 474 counted the refusal as both the third time and the fourth entry, and the state files inherited both.** **The two are not in conflict and never were: three refusals, four pages in books, because the first entry in her life at seventeen was not a refusal.** Xie Changyin's mouth and the paragraph that lists the three prior pages now both say four, the line that did the counting says *Three refusals in eight years, and every one of them went into a book anyway, and this is the fourth page any of them has put in there*, and the closing paragraph stands on four. **`state/current.md`, `state/continuity.md` and `state/chapter-summaries.md` already carried *fourth entry* and are now correct rather than contradicted; the one line that did not — `state/current.md`'s quotation of Xie Changyin — now quotes the chapter.**
3. **ch 470 carried a bare volume citation — `She has known that since Volume 4` — which falsifies a recorded zero, because the pattern the budget row prints cannot match a numeral after the word.** The failure mode this manuscript has already caught itself twice, at ch 417 and in Batch 0003. **Recast to ground: *since she was fourteen*, which is also true of a paper she has carried against her ribs for four years.** **Authorial scaffolding across all fifty-eight chapters of Volume 8 is 0 on the widened pattern, re-measured on the files as they stand after this repair.**

#### The four that should have been fixed

4. **The retired hedged-duration construction survived in recast form** — *about ten seconds* at ch 466, *about four minutes* at ch 473 — and the budget row claimed the family was clean. **Both are plain counts now, and the row above states the true count of what is still standing: nine hedged short durations in the batch, listed.**
5. **ch 474 restated *there is no hand that takes it out* four times**, twice inside four lines and once more in the same paragraph as the first of them. **It now does it twice: once in her mouth, with Xie Changyin's *There is not* under it, and once at the close.** Two further restatements of the same principle were cut.
6. **An unexplained byte-identical duplicate paragraph** — *She is at the top of a realm at eighteen years old.*, at ch 470 and ch 476 — is broken; the ch 476 instance is now *She is eighteen years old and she is at the top of a realm, and that is not the finding*, which is also the better sentence because it sets up the reversal the chapter is built on. **The two duplicates that remain in the batch are deliberate and were always recorded: the bill figure at ch 466/467 and the anonymity line at ch 475/478.**
7. **`eleven` stood eleven times in ch 478 and thirteen in ch 465** and read as a tic at the volume's last page. **Now 9 and 11.** The instances carrying arithmetic were kept — *eight of eleven holds*, *eleven days of not saying a name*, the count itself — and four were cut, including a doubled eleven in one sentence at ch 478 and a sentence in ch 465 that ch 478 repeated almost word for word.

#### The two on process

8. **The volume-close phase was missing.** The Batch 0004 phase wrote `workspace/volume-09-planning/PROMPT.md` where a close prompt belonged. **The writer complied with the prompt it was given; the defect is upstream, in `workspace/volume-08-batch-0004/PROMPT.md`. `workspace/volume-09-planning/` is removed and `workspace/volume-08-close/PROMPT.md` is the one next phase**, following the shape Volumes 5, 6 and 7 each ran. **The concrete cost was not tidiness: with no close phase, Volume 8's fifty-eight-chapter figures are self-certified by the phase that wrote fourteen of the fifty-eight chapters, and the three-batch figure discrepancy below had no phase left to settle it. It is settled here instead, and the close inherits the answer rather than the mystery.**
9. **`state/current.md`'s *Next phase* bullet carried the live prompt and the whole of a superseded one inline**, with the live prompt buried under about two thousand characters of dead prompt text. **Corrected in place: the live next phase is now the first thing the bullet says.**

#### Two defects the review did not find, found while repairing the three above

10. **ch 470 dated the form-reading wrong.** *She spent eight days in a room with a lamp in it on the twelfth of a month five days from now* — which is the twenty-third of the month she is standing in, while the outline's own day table and ch 473 both put the reading on the **first of the month beginning 1,304**, thirteen days after the eighteenth. **Recast: *she is going to sit in a room with a lamp in it on the first of a month and have a document read out loud to her*** — the flash-forward still resolves at ch 474 and no longer misstates the calendar.
11. **ch 476 said she had been at the top of a realm for eleven days.** The stage is at day 1,291 and ch 476's fourteenth is at day 1,317, so it is **twenty-six**. **Corrected, and the second instance at ch 476 recast to *it has been the want since the eighteenth of a month*, which is the date that actually starts it.** Eleven was counted from ch 474 rather than from ch 470; it is the same slip the drafting phase caught and corrected in the opposite direction at ch 465 in its own second sweep.

#### What this repair changed in the figures

**Batch 0004 is 29,125 words of body prose, average ~2,080, and Volume 8 is 107,669, average ~1,856** — the counts as they stood were 29,186 and 107,730. **The method note that accompanied the old figures described a script returning 27,666 / 21,681 / 27,879 for Batches 0001–0003 while the tables carried 28,334 / 21,869 / 28,341, and recorded the 668 / 188 / 462 gap as unexplained. It is explained: the gap is exactly the `##` section-heading word count in each of those three batches, because their own tables were measured with headings excluded and the volume re-measure used a script that counted them.** Both conventions are now stated at §*Established facts, Volume 8 Batch 0004* and §*Volume 8 as a whole, closed, re-measured*, with both sets of figures, because this run has published both and a reader of either alone will think the other is wrong. **No batch figure was ever wrong and no chapter before ch 465 was opened.**

**Also recorded here because it was measured and is nobody's finding yet: twenty-nine chapter files in Volume 8 have no trailing newline — Batch 0001's fourteen and Batch 0003's fifteen.** The Volume 7 close fixed 250 such files as an operational repair. It does not touch a word count and it is not a prose defect. **It is for the Volume 8 close.**

#### What this repair did not do

**It did not re-open a chapter to re-decide a beat, and it did not reopen the review's clean findings.** The review confirmed the refusal at ch 474 as the volume's irreversible step, confirmed that the silence after it is the real cost, and confirmed all fourteen cards land. **It did not audit Volume 8. That is what `workspace/volume-08-close/PROMPT.md` is for, and the three batches that were never audited at volume level are still unaudited.**

---

## Volume 8 close — repairs

**The Volume 8 close phase repaired nothing and says so plainly.** `workspace/volume-08-close/PROMPT.md` gave it
twenty-one findings to record and forbade it from writing chapter prose, and the reason is on the record of this run:
a close that improves a volume by writing prose is a close that has become a rewrite, and the three earlier volumes in
this run each have a record of what that costs. **This section therefore carries a count of zero for chapters written
and a count of twenty-two for findings — the twenty-one the close recorded plus F22, which the close review added
because the close's own method could not see it — and it corrects the state files in place rather than the page.** The
repair to the figures themselves is at `## Volume 8 close review — repair` below this section.

### The twenty-two

**A. The calendar — one defect, twenty-five instances, ten chapters.**

**F1 — Batch 0004's relative-month figures do not reproduce against the volume's own calendar.** The volume counts
elapsed months as month-begins crossed, and the months in Volume 8 begin on days 1,064 · 1,094 · 1,124 · 1,154 ·
1,184 · 1,214 · 1,244 · 1,274 · 1,304 · 1,334. **Seven instances on the page, in six places, get that exactly right and
are the evidence that the convention is the drafters' own**: ch 462 ×2 (both *four months*, both correct against ch 434
at day 1,152 and ch 433 at ~1,142) · ch 465 (*no bread since the twenty-eighth of a month two months back*, day 1,211,
correct) · ch 472 ×2 (*you told me that in a doorway five months ago*, ch 432, correct) · ch 478 (*came open on the
eighteenth of a month two months back*, ch 470 at day 1,291, correct) · and ch 478's *eighty-four days* for the stop at
ch 462, day 1,256, which is 1,340 − 1,256 exactly.

**Twenty-five instances across ten chapters fail — ch 467, 469, 470, 471, 472, 473, 474, 476, 477 and 478, which is every
chapter the table below names and no other.** *(Corrected by the close review: this line previously said fourteen
chapters. The failure table underneath it names ten, and the fourteen was the phase's own working note and not a
measurement.)* The worst is the one the volume's late register rests on:
**the ch 417 refusal is called *four months back* eight times across ch 469, 472, 473 and 474, and ch 417 is the
nineteenth of the month beginning 1,034, which is day 1,052. From ch 474 (day 1,306, the month beginning 1,304) that is
nine months, not four.** The rest, all measured the same way:

| Ch | Instance | Referent | Stated | Actual |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 469 ×3, 472 ×2, 473 ×1, 474 ×2 | *four months* — the ch 417 refusal | ch 417, day 1,052 | four | **nine** |
| 474 | *Five months ago in daylight on a road, to a woman of sixty-one, in silence* | ch 449, day 1,205 | five | **four** |
| 473, 474, 477 ×2 | *a shed at a mill five months back* | ch 425, day 1,103 | five | **seven** |
| 471, 474 | *five months* — the woman of twenty-two at her bench | ch 445, day 1,192 | five | **three** and **four** |
| 470 | *She had learned that in a shed at a mill four months ago, on the twenty-ninth of a month* | ch 434, day 1,152 | four | **five** |
| 470 | *You said a date out loud to a stranger in a doorway four months back* | ch 432, the month beginning 1,124 | four | **five** |
| 467 | *The day anybody found out was the sixth of a month four months back* | ch 421, day 1,099 | four | **six** |
| 470, 476 ×2, 478 | *no bread since the twenty-eighth of a month two months back* | day 1,211 | two | **three**, **four**, **five** |
| 478 | *she asked him to put one down on the thirteenth of a month two months back* | ch 462, day 1,256 | two | **three** |
| 477 | *on the twenty-seventh of a month four months ago with the point of a bar* | ch 466, day 1,270 | four | **two** |
| 471 | *In a shed at a mill, eight months ago* | ch 419, the month beginning 1,064 | eight | **seven** |

**F2 — ch 474 lists the three refusals out of order and with two of three intervals wrong, the two errors pulling in
opposite directions.** The chapter says *Four months ago in a room with a lamp, to a man of forty-three… Five months ago
in daylight on a road, to a woman of sixty-one, in silence. And now…* and then calls the list *Three refusals in eight
years*. **A five-months-back event cannot precede a four-months-back one in a chronological list; neither figure is
right; ch 417 is nine months back and ch 449 is four.** The chapter's substance stands — three refusals, the third of
them hers, the fourth page in a book, the first one she chose — and **the volume's irreversible decision is not
weakened by this finding.**

**F3 — ch 478 carries ch 465's interval forward eighty-one days without re-deriving it.** *No bread since the
twenty-eighth of a month two months back* is exactly right at ch 465 and appears again at ch 470, twice at ch 476 and
at ch 478, where it is five months. **The second time in this run that a carried figure has falsified a recorded zero,
and the first time it has falsified itself inside one volume.**

**THE REPAIR FOR F1, F2 AND F3 IS TEN CHAPTER FILES AND TWENTY-SIX LINES. IT IS A REPAIR PHASE'S AND NOT THIS ONE'S.
*(Corrected by the close review: this line previously said seventeen chapter files. Seven of the seventeen — ch 421, 425,
432, 434, 445, 449 and 466 — appear in the finding table above only as **referents**, the days the wrong figures are
measured against. No wrong figure is on those seven pages, and a repair phase working the old list would have opened
them and changed nothing.)***

**B. Card vocabulary on the page — one defect, three headings.**

**F4 — three section headings are outline instructions**, in the exact class the Volume 7 close recorded as F3 and left
unrepaired: `## His Mouth, In Five Pieces` at ch 457, the **byte-identical** `## His Mouth, In Five Pieces` at ch 461,
and `## The Sixth Mouth` at ch 477. *Mouth* is `outline/volume-08.md`'s own term for a structural-answer beat and *in
five pieces* is the card's phrasing for *four or five short sentences each*; *The Sixth Mouth* is the label on the
outline's six-mouths table row. Volume 7's pages carry `## Mouth Five` (ch 409) and `## Mouth Six` (ch 415) and the
Volume 7 close named this class as the one the Batch 0003 review had already repaired at ch 401 and ch 404. **The class
recurred in Volume 8 and reached Batch 0004, which passed review twice. The repair is three headings.**

**D. Continuity items that hold in a changed form — five.**

- **F11 — the `eighty-four` density.** Measured case-insensitive, whole-word, raw files, **including the ordinal
  `eighty-fourth`: 18 volume-wide, by ch 464 ×12, ch 466, ch 468, ch 470 ×2 and ch 478 ×2.** Measured case-sensitive

  review reduced `eleven` in ch 465 and ch 478 as a tic and did not reduce this, and **ch 464 doubles it inside one
  sentence.** The density finding and the three-to-five-instance repair at ch 464 hold on either method, because ch 464

- **F12 — `erased` is 1, at ch 424**: *The four erased months are the only thing she has ever thrown away and she did

  four erased months are **referred to once, in narration, in her own reasoning about never throwing a line away. No
  month is named, no date given, nobody named, the silence unbroken and nothing forgiven or softened.** The fact holds;
  the measured zero does not carry forward and is not inherited as one.

  going to start.* `Hunter` and `Bai Zhu` both returned 0 across Volumes 6 and 7. **The man with the notebook is named

  she asks him her question in the same scene.** A change, not a breach.

- **F15 — `licence` is 2, at ch 425 and ch 449**, both in Xie Changyin's mouth and both the Order's own paper.

**E. Operational — one.**

**F16 — twenty-nine chapter files have no trailing newline: Batch 0001's fourteen (ch 421–434) and Batch 0003's fifteen
(ch 450–464), none in Batch 0002 or Batch 0004.** The Volume 7 close fixed 250 such files. **It does not touch a word
count. It is how a chapter that ends without a line break collides with the next file in a concatenated read, which is
how two word-count conventions came to disagree by a figure nobody could place. Twenty-nine bytes, for a repair phase.**

**F. Section-heading identity — one defect, one chapter. (F22, added by the close review.)**

- **F22 — `chapters/volume-08/chapter-0451.md` carries `## The Lane End` twice, at lines 101 and 127.** Two consecutive
  sections in one chapter share a title, so a table of contents shows the same name twice under ch 451. **The review's
  finding is that the close could not have seen it, and the reason is the method: the duplicate scan compared
  **body-prose paragraph blocks at six words or more**, and `##` heading lines were outside it by method. **Re-run over
  every block, headings included, the volume returns 15 duplicated texts at 30 instances** — four of those texts are
  `##` heading pairs, all cross-chapter (ch 421/428 *The Yard At The Turn*, ch 436/462 *What She Did Not Do*, ch 457/461
  *His Mouth, In Five Pieces*, ch 461/466 *What She Did Not Say*), each six words or more. **Drop the four and eleven
  texts at twenty-two instances remain, which is F21 and reproduces exactly. Ch 451's `## The Lane End` is three words,
  so it could not have qualified under either reading — the scan's floor was six.** The measurement was sound and simply
  had a floor below the thing it needed to see. F4 shows the phase did read
  every `##` line in the volume, for vocabulary; the hole was in what it compared, not in what it read.
  **Measured as headings, which is the measurement that was missing: across ch 421–478, 22 section-heading texts appear
  more than once, at 45 instances. Exactly one collides inside a single chapter — ch 451.** The other 21 are cross-chapter
  echoes, and most are bookends rather than faults — `## The Shed` at ch 441 and ch 478, `## The Reading` at ch 445 and
  ch 473, `## The Lane` at ch 450 and ch 454, `## The Yard At The Turn` at ch 421 and ch 428, `## The Stone` at ch 427
  and ch 448, `## The Walk Back` at ch 438 and ch 440 — and `## The Arithmetic` is the only text that appears three
  times, at ch 422, 429 and 460. **The repair is one heading and one line, at ch 451, which is already inside the
  apostrophe repair's file range. The cross-chapter echoes are left to a volume-scale pass and are named here so that a
  repair phase does not open twenty-one files chasing them.**

**G. Recorded and not repaired, with the reason — five.**

- **F17 — `about…`, 470 volume-wide**, method B, `\babout\b`, whole word, case-insensitive; **by batch 196 · 61 · 75 ·
  138**, average 8.1 a chapter against Volume 7's 10.1. A character hedge and not a banned word. Recorded rather than
  swept, because the failure this file exists to prevent is a budget that reports a number nobody measured.
- **F18 — `whole of`, 83 volume-wide**, method B, whole word, case-insensitive; **by batch 33 · 17 · 17 · 16**, average
  1.4 against Volume 7's 2.7. The spine of the voice. Xie Changyin's *that is the whole of it* is a character's habit.
- **F19 — the *nobody* family, 221 volume-wide** — nobody 169 · no one 2 · not one 50 — average 3.8 a chapter against
  Volume 7's 0.6. **The outline's own reason holds and is recorded: Volume 8 is the first volume of the run with a large
  number of people in it who do not know her.** Not a licence for it, and not swept.
- **F20 — `eleven`, 229 volume-wide**, densest at ch 452 with 17, ch 423 with 12, ch 465 and ch 470 with 11. It is the
  stage word (*eight of eleven holds*) and it is load-bearing. Measured and recorded.
- **F21 — eleven duplicated paragraph texts at 22 instances**, of which two are planted and recorded (the bill at ch
  466/467, the anonymity line at ch 475/478), one is a carried beat (*He is not gone*, ch 463/478), **one is a verbatim
  restatement of the ch 434 cost — *She can still say no. She just cannot make the no take.* — byte-identical at ch 460
  and ch 470** — and seven are furniture.

### The hand-off, and what the close must not preview

**`outline/volume-08.md` §*New question for Volume 9* is fixed and is not previewed, expanded or softened here.**
What the audit confirms is the narrower thing that matters: **Volume 8 hands Volume 9 its antagonist without putting one
word of the Warden's office, the list, or the Anchor on its page, and the new question is a consequence of what ch
462–478 resolved.** `Shuyin` returns **0** across ch 421–478. Nothing in this close spends anything `outline/ending.md`
§1 owns, and Volume 9 opens on **day 1,341, the eighth of the month beginning 1,334**, derived under
`((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` and not asserted.

### The volume's own answer, and this close's verdict on it

**She read the sheet to them first, and then she said no, and a man of sixty-one wrote one line and the date in the
ordinary hand. It went in. There is no hand in that building or on that ground that takes it out. It is the fourth
entry of her life and the first one she chose, and the price of it is not punishment: the ground went quiet, and at the
last page nothing on nine days of road wants anything from her at all.**

**Ch 474 says it and ch 478 says it and both are true. No finding in this audit weakens any of it.** The month figures
in F1, F2 and F3 are connective tissue and touch no beat, no figure of hers, no refusal and no silence. The card
headings in F4 are three headings. The apostrophes in F8 are thirty-three bytes in Batch 0003. **Where the page is
right, the page is right.**

## Volume 9 Batch 0001 (ch 479–493) — established facts, carried to Batch 0002

**Ch 479–493 are on the page.** Fifteen chapters, 27,725 words of body prose on method B (every line not beginning
`#`), average ~1,848, day 1,341 to day 1,412. The drafting phase is `.deferred` and was not marked complete; the
chapters are finished and were verified clean by the review of `volume-09-planning`. **This section is the batch's
hand-off, written by the planning phase's review fix.**

- **The days.** ch 479 on day 1,341 through ch 493 on day 1,412, month-days derived under `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1`
  with months beginning 1,334 · 1,364 · 1,394. The five anchors the batch prompt pinned all hold: ch 484 on the
  second of the month beginning 1,364, ch 485 on the seventh, ch 487 on the seventeenth, **ch 488 on the twenty-second,
  which is day 1,385**, and **ch 493 on the nineteenth, which is day 1,412.** ch 493 closes in the morning after, on the
  twentieth, at the seventh hour — that is day 1,413, and it is the first hour of Batch 0002's range and not a
  contradiction of the batch's closing day.
- **Severance 1 is taken at ch 493, day 1,412, the fourth hour**, at the ceiling-breaking stage of this realm, without
  a tribulation, without a bracket, and announced by nothing but the chapter. **121 days after Nascent Soul 9 at day
  1,291; 149 days before Severance 3 at day 1,561; the three stages stand 270 days apart.**
- **Severance 1's cost, named once at ch 493: she cannot keep a place by staying in it.** The place is the shed, not a
  limb and not a hand. **No later chapter may re-derive it.**
- **The paper against her ribs is open.** Opened alone at ch 488, day 1,385, at the fifth hour, with the door barred.
  **It has never been named by a trade term and must not be.** *Foundation the first stage* is on the front of it in a
  hand that is not hers. She read the inside at ch 489 and told nobody what it says. **Twenty-seven days separate the
  opening on day 1,385 from the stage on day 1,412.**
- **The silence has now run eighty-five days as of ch 479, counted on the page.** Day 1,256 (ch 462, where she makes
  him stop) to day 1,340 (ch 478) is the pinned **eighty-four**; ch 479 opens on day 1,341 and the page says
  **eighty-five**. **Both figures are correct for their own day and neither may be used for the other.** Chapter 479
  derives the subtraction on the page.
- **He is absent from all fifteen chapters.** His name does not appear and he does not appear. The frame of his
  silence is unchanged and Volume 9 does not break it until ch 520.
- **The Hunter is on the page once, at ch 491**, nine feet off the side of a track with a notebook shut under his arm,
  and she stops twelve feet off. He answers her and **does not write it down.** He is twice in Volume 9 and only
  twice; **one appearance remains, and she asks him both times.**

### One contradiction the review did not name, found while closing F1, and closed with it

**The outline carried two different silence anchors and no rule for which figure used which.** `## Starting
relationships` said he *"has said nothing since ch 471"*, which is true — he speaks in both ch 466 and ch 471, and ch
471 is the last room he is in with her — while the escalation counted the run from day 1,256, which is ch 462. Left
alone, the two would have produced two different answers for the same question, and a Batch 0003 drafter computing *over
two hundred days* would have had a figure of 232 on one reading and 273 on the other. **The outline now keeps them
apart: everything measured as *nothing to spend* is counted from day 1,256, everything measured as *said nothing* is
counted from ch 471, and the outline's own eighty-four, eighty-five, 265 and 273 are all on the first.** It also
records what that count does not include: **the frame scene at ch 518 is away from her hearing and is not in it**, which
is what lets him speak there and still have ch 520 be the first time he speaks to her.
