### Established facts

- **Severance 2 is taken at ch 508, day 1,488, the fourth hour**, on open flat ground, alone, with no wall and no door
  within nine hundred yards, no tribulation, and the batch's one bracketed layer line. **Seventy-six days after Severance 1
  at day 1,412** and **three hundred and twenty-one days before Severance 3 at day 1,561**; 1,488 − 1,412 = 76 and
  1,561 − 1,488 = 73. **The volume's three stages now stand 149 days apart, first to third.**
- **Severance 2's cost, named once at ch 508: she cannot be met where she is expected.** A person who comes for her comes
  to the place she was, and the place she was is a line on a page. **Not a limb, not a hold, not a count of breath, and
  not a building.** **No later chapter may re-derive it.** Severance 1's cost was named once at ch 493 and is carried here
  only as a fact, never re-derived.
- **The question she asked at the fourth hour of the fifth of a month, out loud, once, in the ordinary voice, was *what am
  I at*.** It is the question she has been asking for eight years — of forty-one lines a month, of a rate behind a
  counter, of a bench and a trough road — and it has answered with work every time. **It came back with a shed: the shed
  at the end of the lane, by name, as a page names a place.** That is the first non-work answer the reach has ever
  returned and it is the engine of ch 508.
- **The paper against her ribs is open, read, unnamed, and is not the door.** Opened alone at ch 488, read at ch 489.
  **She said so out loud to Shuyin's face at ch 503** — *that is the only door in the world* — and he answered *no*, once,
  in one word, and then would not be drawn further. **It is still folded in four against her ribs and has still never been
  called by any trade term, and no card may call it one.**
- **Shuyin is established on the page and is correct.** Named once, at ch 498, out loud through a plank door, and not
  repeated. **Sits on the ground outside a shut door for two hours and waits for the latch before he does anything else
  at all.** Gets up off flat ground in one movement without a hand. Weight on one leg. **No staff, no court, no office,
  no uniform, no wing, no rank list, no committee, no house — one book, one rule, one lane.** He is not in a room in this
  batch: the outline gives Batch 0002 two walks to her and gives his room to Batch 0004. **He asks her no question about
  herself in fifteen chapters**, buys nothing, threatens nothing, lies about nothing that matters, and does not prefer
  kept to lost.
- **The rule, stated in full inside six chapters (ch 498–504), and not again in this batch:** a name goes in the book when
  a person gets to the top of a realm with no document to take them past it; a name is written once, as kept or as lost;
  the rule is older than she is; **the bound goes around a list rather than through it and does not know the list is
  there; and there is no reason given for any of it.** **His argument is spent once, at ch 499, and is not repeated:**
  *Somebody has to write down the ones the bound takes, or there is no record that the bound takes any. I write them. I
  have written them for nine hundred years. The writing does not save one of them and I have never once said it did.*
- **What he will not say is established and is not to be paid again in this volume.** She asks *why does a bounded world
  need readers* once, at ch 499. He does not answer, does not explain, and at ch 503 says a second time that he has a
  version and it belongs to somebody who is not standing there. **The origin and the moral answer are not on the page and
  Shuyin is not allowed to become the thing that explains the Wall.** **The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the
  crews wrong.**
- **THE BATCH'S IRREVERSIBLE STEP — ch 502.** She said, out loud, in daylight, on a bench at a crossing, with a boy of
  seventeen forty feet off, the words she read back to a man behind a counter in a room with the shutters up two months
  ago: *Ning Yan. Present, at the fourth hour, in the room, on the third of this month, brought in by the terms of her own
  asking. The terms have been read to her at the fourth hour in the room with the man who keeps the book at the end of
  the table. She has read the paper aloud to the room. She has declined it. Nothing has been asked of her twice.* **He wrote
  it in his own hand, in forty seconds, with the date, and with the place she named — the shed at the end of the lane.**
  She did not offer to write it herself and did not let him add kept or lost. **It is a page about her, not the entry;
  the entry proper — kept or lost — is Batch 0004's at ch 536, and the rule is *written once*, so nothing in Batch 0003 may
  write it.** **It cannot be un-copied, and no card in Batch 0003 may soften, defer or un-let it.**
- **The mechanism, established at ch 503 and load-bearing for ch 508: he used the date, not the place.** A page with a
  stage in it on one date in one month narrows nine days of road to a handful of people, and he said so in eleven seconds
  and said he had walked the lane four times and had not once used the place. **So she gave a page away and it did not
  save him a mile, and it is now the only thing in the world that can find her.**
- **His own entry is on the page (ch 500).** He is in the book, first page, **kept**, nine hundred years, the only entry
  he has ever been able to check, and *it has not saved one of them and it is still the correct line.* **He does not know
  how many names are in it and has been asked four times and given the same answer four times.**
- **`Warden` is his only title and it is used twice in the batch** (ch 500, given by him, once in dialogue and once as
  the word on the road; ch 504, in her own counting). **`Shuyin` stands 1**, at ch 498. **`list` stands 10** and is
  load-bearing; the book is a list and he says so.
- **The three structural mouths the outline names for this batch are both spent, in their pinned chapters.** **Ch 497, a
  man of fifty who has carried boards across that lane and up the long road for nine years and has been written down
  twice, once eleven years apart, and was never read to either time** — *being written without being read is an ordinary
  thing past the turnstile and it is only strange to the person it happens to*, in five sentences. **Ch 505, the keeper
  with the paste pot and the rag at the post on the strip road nine miles west, with a ladder and a flat stone and a book
  on it that has three names in it in nineteen years, every one of them put there by himself** — *no office knows it is
  there and it is going to be here after I am dead and whoever has this post next will not know to look*, in five
  sentences. **Neither is a villain and neither is made wrong. Neither has said why. `Shuyin` is mouth five at ch 531 and
  is unspent.**
- **Wei Ran: one of Volume 9's two cards, in this batch.** **Nine coppers a head, and the bar goes in the cart and does
  not go in the rate — said out loud three times**, twice to Ning Yan (ch 497, ch 505) and once to a woman on the front
  bench (ch 505). **She carried her twice**: four miles west to the store on the seventh, and nine miles west to the post
  on the eighteenth. **Eight coppers remain on her book from eight months back and she has never chased them and did not
  chase these either.** *She says a rate the same way to everybody and that is what a rate is.* **No romance, no harem,
  nothing but a trade, a team, a rate and an opinion.**
- **Zheng, thirty-four, of the frame at the cut, is used and not spent.** Fitted at ch 495, mould scraped at ch 503, and
  he has a bar on his own leg now. **He does not know what is at the end of the lane and is not going to ask.**
- **The woman at the store five miles west is used and not spent.** Ch 506: **she put a loaf down and said *no* on her own
  counter for the first time in thirty years, and did not know that Ning Yan cannot take anything**, and said *if you
  have not got anywhere to go, there is a room at the end of the lane behind the bench shed. I know the woman. You have
  only to say.* **The bread the man who stopped coming did not pay for is still on the shelf and is still not given away
  and is still not eaten.**
- **The woman of fifty at the shed at the end of the lane is used and not spent.** Ch 507: she told her the bench was empty
  on the day and not before, exactly as she said in a month that has gone, and then offered her the frame room and a bolt
  on the west door without asking. **She is correct and she is the fourth person to be wrong about her.**
- **The boy at the crossing is seventeen and is used as furniture, not as a mouth.** **The ch 514 mouth of Batch 0003 is a
  boy of fifteen and must be a different boy — put him at the gate with the chain and the cup, not at this crossing.**
- **THE MISREADING, established at ch 504 and running.** The carrier stopped saying good on the ninth; a carter of
  twenty-two pulled his horse up two hundred yards short of her on the eleventh; the woman at the store on the
  twenty-third; the woman of fifty on the second of the next month. **Eleven people on nine days of road have decided
  that something has come for her and that she is the one inside it, and every one of them is correct about the shape and
  wrong about the person, and it is the first time in a year that a thing about her has been misread on this ground.**
  **She has decided not to correct it, out loud, twice, because correcting it means the page and there is nowhere on
  nine days of road she can put the page.** The carrier said good again on the fourteenth, on purpose, for her.
  **The outline gives this two months and it runs past the end of this batch: it is four days old at ch 508 and it must
  reach its full height in Batch 0003.**
- **The reach, the body and the means at ch 508.** **Right index gone, four fingers and a thumb. Right arm open four days
  in five.** Eight of eleven holds. **Short breath four counts everywhere including inside a loaded reach.** The open
  place low behind her fourth rib, the size of a coin, since the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,274, **and it is what
  the merit comes out of.** Not hungry no more and that is finished; thinner than a month ago. **Bar, strap bound out of
  her own coat lining; dish with clay; no money, no wages, no bread since the second of a month; sleeping in the open in
  the lee of a low bank. Eighteen years old.**
- **Merit settles once in this batch, at ch 495**, on listed work cleared past the line — a leg fitted on Zheng's stump —
  and **not on a death.** It comes up out of the ground under her feet out of the open place behind her fourth rib and
  goes into a list in the ground that any hand anywhere can go looking for work in, and **it does not come back**, and
  **no figure of it and no figure of what is left of him is stated on any page before it or after it.**
- **He is absent from all fifteen chapters and his name does not appear.** **The run from day 1,256 is unbroken and stands
  at 232 days at ch 508** (1,488 − 1,256). **Measured as *said nothing* it runs from ch 471 and Batch 0002 does not use
  that figure.** **No Hand is spent. No Door is opened. `Ledger` returns 0.**
- **The Hunter is not on the page in this batch.** One appearance remains in Volume 9, at Batch 0004, and she asks him.
  **His name was spent at ch 491 and may not be used again.**

a romance · **she never says her mother's name** · no new final enemy · **no duplicate `##` heading in this batch.**

### Three readings taken against the outline, recorded rather than hidden

1. **Ch 508's shed.** The outline pins, in the midpoint section and again in escalation item 8, *a man of nine hundred
   years walks four miles east of a mill to a shed with boards on the floor.* The state file puts **the lane-end group
   thirteen miles east of the shed at the mill**, and the outline's own `## Major locations` gives the Warden's room as
   **past the turnstile** and gives his room to the batches that come after this one. **The scene as pinned is kept in
   full — he shuts a door, he waits for the latch, she is not in it, and the cost lands — and the shed is the shed at the
   end of the lane**, which is the place on her page and the place she has left. **The turnstile is not crossed in this
   batch**, which keeps `## Major locations`'s *Volume 9 crosses past it once and does not come back the same way* whole
   for the crossing at ch 518. **No chapter states a distance from a mill that contradicts either reading.**
2. **The two-month misreading clock.** Escalation item 6 gives *two months*, counted from ch 498 at day 1,440, which
   falls in Batch 0003 at about ch 510. **The misreading is established and at full local height inside this batch and is
   carried live; the full two months is a Batch 0003 beat and no card in Batch 0003 may treat Batch 0002 as having
   already spent it.**
3. **The order of the copy and the finding.** The outline's continuity item 17 says her ch 474 refusal, copied by another
   hand, *is the reason the list found her*, while the same outline puts the finding at ch 498 and the copy at ch 502. **The
   page reconciles and the batch follows the page:** he finds her by walking and asking, and the copy at ch 502 is what
   makes her *findable by a list and not by a road*, which is exactly what Severance 2 costs her at ch 508. **Nothing in
   Batch 0003 may put the copy before ch 502 or treat the lane-end search as unnecessary.**

## Volume 9 Batch 0002 (ch 494–508) — REVIEW REPAIR

**Three chapters reopened, three defects closed, no beat, day, title, stage, bracket or planned event moved.** The review
found the mechanical layer exact — 21 forbidden terms at 0 across the fifteen files, one bracketed layer line and it is
at ch 508, zero blockquotes, code blocks, tables, rules, bold spans and italic spans, every chapter inside 1,800–2,700
words, and both named cards (ch 497 and ch 505) landed whole — and it found one missing deliverable and three text
defects. **The missing deliverable is closed at the foot of this section. The three defects are closed here.**

1. **Ch 507's own chronology.** The line *Nineteen days ago somebody wrote down where I sleep* contradicted the batch's
   own frame. Ch 507 sets the arithmetic on **the third at the shed, in the afternoon**, and ch 508 states in its own
   words that *the page was written by her out loud on a bench at a crossing on the third of a month in daylight with a
   boy forty feet off*; ch 508 also gives *three nights* out of the shed and the state file gives the misreading four days
   old at ch 508. **Nineteen was the only figure in the batch with no other file behind it.** It now reads *This morning
   somebody wrote down where I sleep. I have not slept in it since and I am not going to.* — **the third of the month
   beginning 1,484, at about the seventh hour, on the page, and the last clause sets up ch 508, where the reach returns
   the shed anyway and she is four hundred yards off it.** The prose around it is untouched and the arithmetic block at
   ch 507 is unchanged.
2. **Ch 502's retrospective elapsed-time markers.** Four markers — *in the three days since*, *for three days*,
   *for four days*, *for four days* — described time passing since a crossing the chapter itself dates to **the third at
   the seventh hour**, with the price named at the second hour of the fourth and the boy on the chain in the morning
   after. **One day had elapsed and the chapter said three and four.** They now read *in the hours since*, *since the
   seventh hour of the third*, *since the third*, and the closing one carries it in the other shape — *she has not been
   able to put it anywhere else since the third* — **so that the date is not struck twice in the same construction in the
   same closing movement.** **No date in the chapter moved; only the four retrospective markers describing the interval
   between them.**
3. **Ch 505's doubled beat.** *She had it in her hand to ask him for the stone and she did not ask* stood twice within
   three lines, once with the reasoning and once without. **It is now stated once, with the reasoning, and broken at
   the beat: the sentence about nine seconds of looking at a book on a stone, and then the short plain line — *She did
   not ask, and it is the hardest thing she has not done in eight years, and she wants it in the page in her own words
   and not as a virtue.*** The chapter's best line survives whole and the chapter is thirteen words shorter on method B,
   **1,865 to 1,852**.

**Re-measured after the repair, method B, on the fifteen files as they now stand: 28,532 words, average ~1,902, range
1,767 at ch 496 to 2,133 at ch 500** — **the batch total is unchanged, and that is a measurement and not a
coincidence: the three files moved by −13 at ch 505, +8 at ch 502 and +5 at ch 507 on method B, a net of zero, so every
figure the drafting phase published for Batch 0002 stands without correction.** Brackets: still **1** in fifteen chapters,
at ch 508, and nothing else in square brackets. Forbidden terms: still **0** across all 21. Duplicate paragraphs,
section-heading collisions, straight quotes and straight apostrophes: unchanged at 0 on the multi-line measure that has
been used all volume.

### The one contradiction the review did not name, found while measuring the repair

**`state/open-threads.md` §*What Batch 0003 inherits* said *The Hunter's second and last appearance is in this batch and
she asks him*, and it is false.** His first page was ch 491 and his second is Batch 0004's, at the mouth of ch 531, as
`outline/volume-09.md` §*The Hunter in Volume 9* and `state/continuity.md` both state. A drafter reading that sentence
would have spent the Hunter on the wrong ground and collided with ch 531. **The line is corrected in place** and the
correction is recorded here rather than made silently.

### The staging of the wall, the chair and the hut — pinned for Batch 0003

**The outline fixes the mechanism, the three pinned days and the two silence figures, and leaves the ground to the batch.
This is Batch 0003's own reading and it is now written down so the batch does not have to derive it and Batch 0004
inherits it whole.**

- **The wall is the bound and it is a physical thing**, east of the gate with the chain and the cup, at the end of the
  long road, past the turnstile, **with a hut behind it.** The turnstile is not the wall; the turnstile is how people get
  past.
- **The room is the Warden's room** — one small room, a door that shuts, a chair, a table, a book, no hall in it — **against
  the near face of that wall.** Batch 0004's demand at ch 531 is still Batch 0004's and is not spent here.
- **She goes past the turnstile once, at ch 518, and this batch does not bring her back across it.** `## Major locations`
  gives *Volume 9 crosses past it once and does not come back the same way*, and Batch 0002 left it uncrossed for this.
- **The one who stays wrong-sided is him.** A wall opened from the inside is a document read from the inside by the person
  it is about, without anybody qualified in the room; he reads it; he stays. **The price is a number of one and the
  number is not her**, and she never learns which one he means, because at ch 516 he writes a figure on a page and says
  nothing at all.
- **Ch 519's empty chair is that room's chair**, empty because he is behind a wall he opened for her, with a light in
  the hut behind it for the whole night.
- **Ch 520 is on her side of the turnstile, in daylight, at the gate, with witnesses she did not ask for.** She crosses
  the opened wall in both directions and is not charged for it. **He cannot.**

### The silence, measured honestly, because two copies of it now exist

The outline publishes **265 days at ch 518 and 273 at ch 520, both counted from day 1,256**, and says his silence with
her is unbroken across them. **On the page, Batch 0002's ch 498, 501, 502 and 503 have him speaking to her outdoors — a
shed, a lane, a bench at a crossing, in daylight.** That prose stands and is not to be rewritten. **The two are kept
apart rather than collapsed:** the figure this volume publishes is a run of days from day 1,256, 1,521 − 1,256 = 265 and
1,529 − 1,256 = 273, **and the on-page rule Batch 0003 enforces is the narrower one — no spoken word of his reaches her
hearing in any chapter of Batch 0003 before ch 520, and ch 516 gives him none at all.** The frame scene at ch 518 is away
from her hearing and is not counted against either figure.

### Established facts

- **Severance 3 is taken at ch 523, day 1,561, the fourth hour**, alone, on open flat ground at a turn between the lane
  and the store road, **with no wall and no door within nine hundred yards and no person within nine hundred yards**, no
  tribulation, no clerk, and the batch's one bracketed layer line. **Seventy-three days after Severance 2 at day 1,488**,
  the shortest interval of the run and the shortest since the second stage of the realm, and the chapter says why it is
  short. **1,561 − 1,488 = 73; the three stages now stand 149 days apart first to third and 270 days from Nascent Soul 9
  at day 1,291.**
- **Severance 3's cost, named once at ch 523: she cannot go back down the road she came.** Not Lowwater, not the mill,
  not the store — **the two miles of lane between the bench and the store stop being a distance she can walk. She can
  walk them; they do not go anywhere she has been.** **Not a limb, not a hold, not a count of breath and not a
  building.** Severance 1's and Severance 2's costs are carried at ch 523 only as facts and are not re-derived.
  **No later chapter may re-derive this one.**
- **The price of a door, published in writing at ch 516 and not said.** A single figure on a clean page — one stroke
  standing on its own foot with a flag on it, in the hand nine hundred years old — **and the page has no heading, no
  date, no name and no second line.** She reads it without touching it, does not ask a second time, **and leaves it on
  the bench in the dew**, because her hand will not take a gift. **The price is a number of one. It is not her.**
- **THE BATCH'S IRREVERSIBLE STEP — ch 518.** She says *Open it* out loud, once, in the ordinary voice, on the boards of
  a nine-foot room with the door shut and the latch down and a bar of a wall across the back of it, **knowing the number
  and not knowing whose it is**, and he opens it from the inside. **A wall opened from the inside is a document read
  from the inside by the person it concerns, and there has to be one pair of hands in the room, and he read it.** He
  steps through and **one person stays on the wrong side, permanently, and it is him.** **No card may soften it, defer
  it, or put the man back through the wall.**
- **THE FIFTH FRAME SCENE, at ch 518, inside the opening and away from her hearing.** **It is not a field and it is not
  a list and it is not a strip of paper on a floor; it is a doorway standing in a space with nothing under his feet, four
  feet wide and six feet high, made of pale stone, with one figure standing on the far side of it nine feet tall.** He
  has one question — *Does a wall open from the inside without somebody staying on the wrong side of it* — and he finds
  the answer: **it cannot, and the answer is the figure he put on a page on the first of a month.** **Every sentence he
  speaks in it is in the present tense** and **no other character is in a marked present frame in the batch at all.**
  The frame is **not** counted against his silence with her.
- **The silence, both figures published on the page with the subtraction in the same sentence:** **two hundred and
  fifty-eight days at ch 516** (1,514 − 1,256), **two hundred and sixty-five at ch 518** (1,521 − 1,256), **two hundred
  and seventy-three at ch 520** (1,529 − 1,256). All three are counted from day 1,256, which is ch 462, the chapter where
  she makes him stop and he puts a thing down and does not pick it up again. **On the page, no spoken word of his reaches
  her hearing in any chapter of this batch before ch 520.** Ch 515 and ch 516 give him no line of speech at all; ch 518
  gives him none in the room and his two lines in the frame are away from her hearing. **His first words to her since
  ch 471 are four words at ch 520: *It was mine.*** Batch 0002's ch 498, 501, 502 and 503 have him speaking to her
  outdoors and that prose stands and is not to be rewritten.
- **The wall, the room, the turnstile and the hut, as staged in this batch and now on the page.** **The long road is
  eleven miles from the crossing to the gate** — derived on the page at ch 514 with a nine-foot-nine bar used as the
  measure — **the gate carries a chain and a tin cup on a wire and a boy of fifteen works it from the fourth hour to the
  seventh and from the eleventh to the third; four hundred yards east of the gate is a bar on two posts with nothing on
  it; three hundred yards east of that, in open flat, a wall set in the ground, four feet high, running north and south
  further than the flat goes; a hut a hundred yards behind the wall with one small window and a light in it.** The
  room is built against the **west face** of the wall: **nine feet by eleven, boards on the floor, a chair, a table, a
  book on the table, a bed frame with nothing on it, a shelf with a jug and a folded blanket, a window facing west, and
  a chimney that draws.** **No hall in it and nothing in it that is called anything.**
- **The opening.** He put his two hands flat on the stone four feet off the boards, his shoulders moved once, **there was
  no sound**, the ground inside the wall went down four inches and came up the way clay comes up out of a dish, and there
  is an opening four feet wide and six feet high with a jamb on each side and nothing loose anywhere on it. **She goes
  through it and it costs her nothing and it is not counted for her.** **He cannot come back across it.**
- **She went past the bar on two posts once, at the second hour of the eighth of the month beginning 1,514, and has not
  gone back through it.** Days 1,522–1,524, 1,530–1,531 and 1,557–1,560 are walked or stood out on the page and are not
  gaps. **Nothing in this batch brings her back across it.**
- **Shuyin, in this batch, is a receiver and not a walker.** He is found, and she comes to him. **He says no word in
  her hearing in any chapter before ch 520.** He shuts every door he walks through and waits for the latch — twice on the
  page in this batch, at the trough shed on the thirtieth and in the room at the ninth hour of the eighth. He carries no
  staff, holds no court, wears no uniform, sends for nobody, asks her no question about herself, threatens nobody, and
  **is not a villain and is not made wrong.** He does not explain the wall and he is not allowed to. **`Shuyin` is not
  named in this batch at all, in narration or otherwise, and `Warden` returns 0.**
- **Structural mouth four of five, spent at ch 514**, in a boy of fifteen at the chain, in five short sentences across
  four minutes: *The man that had this chain is not here. / He went in the spring. / Nobody put anybody else on it. / The
  chain goes up and the chain goes down. / There is not an office here. There is a chain and a cup and me.* **He is not
  the boy of seventeen at the crossing, the two never appear in one scene, and he is a mouth and not an office.**
  **The fifth mouth is Batch 0004's, at ch 531, and it is Shuyin's.**
- **The misreading, run to its full height and ended on the page at ch 521.** **Eleven people on nine days of road, two
  months, every one of them correct about the shape and wrong about the person, and not one of them a villain.** It ends
  **because the truth turned out to look like the misreading** — a wall came out of a room past a turnstile and a man did
  not come back out of it — **and not because anybody was corrected.** She corrects nothing and takes no credit for it.
  **On the page:** the carrier of four boards comes down off the lane to ten feet and says good instead of thirty; the
  carter of twenty-two slows to a walk for nine seconds alongside her and asks nothing; the woman at the store puts a
  loaf on the counter and pushes it four inches towards her and does not take it off again; the woman of fifty offers a
  third time and shuts her door for the first time; the man of fifty asks a plain question at the crossing and gives three
  facts. **The bread the man who stopped coming did not pay for is still on the shelf behind the counter and is still not
  given away.** **She is nineteen at ch 520 and at ch 523.**
- **The work, the board and the six day books.** The room with six day books is off the cut on the low side. A woman of
  forty-one keeps them. **The board outside carries forty-one lines a month and she can take none of them, and no line
  out of forty-one has ever had her name on it.** **A space on the board means a thing is wanted and has not been
  taken**, one appeared on the twelfth, and by ch 521 it had a man's name under it. **A line on a frame is a mark on the
  upright at the eighth foot; above it one man carries it alone; four men had gone above it in six weeks and two of them
  brought a stone to the mark and could not get it over.** She carried it on the twelfth, on a body at nine days without
  bread, and her fourth count went at the tenth foot and took four seconds to come back.
- **The merit settles once in this batch, at ch 512, on listed work cleared past the line and not on a death.** It comes
  up out of the ground under her feet out of the open place behind her fourth rib and **it goes down, not up**: into the
  ground and on into the ground's own record of work, which any hand anywhere can go looking for work in. **It does not
  come back.** **The work went in and the man did not: no room, no rate, no woman and no figure, because a day book
  takes a name and a man and a figure and there was no name and no man.** **No figure of the settling is given and no
  figure of what is left of him is stated on any page before it or after it. No Hand is spent. No Door is spent except
  the one at ch 518. `Ledger` returns 0.**
- **The reach, the body and the means at ch 523.** **Right index gone, four fingers and a thumb. Right arm open four
  days in five, and it is a rate and not an event.** Eight of eleven holds. **Short breath four counts everywhere
  including inside a loaded reach, and the fourth count takes about four seconds to come back on flat ground and nine on
  a loaded one, and five seconds at the fourth hour on a flat floor.** **The open place low behind her fourth rib, the
  size of a coin, since the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,274, and it is what the merit comes out of, and it does
  not close.** Not hungry, and that is finished, and she is thinner than she was on the eighth. **Nineteen years old, past
  the ceiling by three stages. No money and no wages for four months. Bar, strap rebound off her own coat lining. Dish
  with clay, ring a finger’s width, both in a bag.** **The paper against her ribs is open, read, unnamed, is not the
  door, and is not called anything in this batch.**
- **The body, as a count of what it has left.** She is at the top of a realm with nowhere to stand past it except through
  a document she opened herself. She cannot be paid. Her hand will not take a gift. She cannot be met where she is
  expected. **And now the road behind her does not go back.** None of the eight costs in eight years is a limb except
  the finger, and none of the three Severance costs touches her body at all.

### Three readings taken against the outline, recorded rather than hidden

1. **The long road is eleven miles, not four.** `outline/volume-09.md` §*Major locations* and ch 504, which is on the
   page and was not reopened, give the long road as going **east for eleven miles to a gate with a chain on it**. The
   batch prompt’s physical-risk line and its ch 514 card both say *a walk of four miles east to a gate*. **The page
   governs, the batch uses eleven, and ch 514 publishes the figure with its method on the page** — twenty-three hundred
   and something lays of a bar nine feet nine — so nothing in the batch contradicts ch 504 and the reader is given a
   measurement rather than a distance asserted. The four-mile figure is not carried.
2. **Ch 520 does not name the third cost.** The ch 520 card’s information line ends *and the road behind her is not
   hers any more.* **Severance 3’s cost is named once, in its own stage chapter, at ch 523, and the outline’s own rule
   is that no later chapter re-derives one.** Ch 520 carries her age, the three stages, the wages, the food and the body
   in full and leaves the road to ch 523.
3. **Where the demand at its maximum is made in Batch 0004.** The man on the wrong side of a wall he opened cannot come
   back across it, so he cannot be in a room on her side of it. **Ch 530–533 will therefore be made at the opened
   wall, in daylight, with his book on a plank table carried out to the near side and him standing on his own side of
   the opening; she does not cross back; and at ch 536 he writes, shuts the book, and walks her to the door of the room
   and stays where he is.** Recorded here so Batch 0004 does not draft a contradiction and then repair it. **No chapter
   in this batch puts him back on her side of the wall and no card in Batch 0004 may.**

## Volume 9 Batch 0003 (ch 509–523) — REVIEW REPAIR (applied after `logs/volume-09-batch-0003.review.log`)

**Four chapters touched, six findings closed, one seventh found while applying them, and no beat, day, title, stage,
bracket, irreversible decision or planned event moved.** The review found the mechanical layer exact and reproducible
under method B — 28,541 words at the time, average ~1,903, range 1,602 to 2,409, fifteen `#` headings with zero
collisions and a longest of 30 characters, zero blockquotes, code blocks, tables and horizontal rules, zero bold and zero
italic, zero straight quotes and zero straight apostrophes, one bracketed layer line at ch 523, `Ledger` 0 · `Warden` 0 ·
`Shuyin` 0 · `vouch` 0 · `about` 72, a longest paragraph of 88 words, the day table re-deriving under
`((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` for every row including both month rollovers, ch 518's irreversible step real and unsoftened,
and ch 520's age turn landing on the page. **All of that reproduces after this repair, and the batch's own claim of *0
exact duplicate paragraphs at six words or more* is now true where it was not.**

### The six findings

1. **F1 — the interval that did not compute.** `state/continuity.md` published `1,516 − 1,256 = 258` twice, in the
   line that opens *every interval the batch publishes is derived in the same sentence that publishes it* and in the
   bullet that publishes the three silences. **The table six lines above pins ch 516 at day 1,514 and
   `chapter-0516.md:23` reads *Five hundred and fourteen less two hundred and fifty-six is two hundred and fifty-eight*.**
   The chapter was right and the operand had been transposed. **Both occurrences now read `1,514 − 1,256`.** No figure
   moved; 258 was always correct.
2. **F2 — the ch 522 row, short by three days.** The row read `1,542–1,553`. `chapter-0522.md:3` has her go east on the
   twenty-ninth (= 1,542), **be at the wall by the second of the next one (= 1,545)**, and **stand at the fourth hour
   every morning for twelve days, which is 1,545 through 1,556** — and `chapter-0523.md:3` opens on the fourteenth
   (= 1,557), which only closes if 1,556 is the last morning stood. **The row now reads `1,542–1,556`, twenty-ninth –
   thirtieth of the month beginning 1,514, then first – thirteenth of the month beginning 1,544**, and every day in it
   re-derives under the month rule.
3. **F3 — the two carried-days notes, which contradicted each other and the table.** The review's suggested value was
   `1,554–1,556`. **That was derived from the stale `state/chapter-summaries.md` row, which pinned ch 523 at
   1,554–1,561, and it cannot stand alongside its own F2**: once the ch 522 row ends at 1,556, the days 1,554–1,556 are
   inside it and are not gaps at all. **The days that are genuinely unpinned after F2 are 1,557–1,560**, and all four are
   walked and dated inside ch 523 — the fourteenth (she leaves the far ground at the fourth hour), the fifteenth (the
   gate at the eleventh hour), the sixteenth (the lane at the crossing at the seventh hour, the loaf on the stone wall,
   the bench at the ninth hour) and the seventeenth (the two miles walked four times, and the cost said out loud at the
   ninth hour). **Both notes now carry 1,557–1,560**, and the first keeps 1,542's run into 1,544, which is a month
   rollover and not a gap. **The review's two findings were applied as one coherent fix rather than as two literal ones,
   and the deviation is recorded here rather than hidden.**
4. **F4 — four stale headers in `state/current.md`.** The `Volume:` bullet had been corrected in place and four other
   places still named Batch 0003 as unwritten. **The `As of:` line, the `Chapter written:` bullet, the `Next phase:`
   bullet and the `In-world date:` bullet now all read ch 523, day 1,561, the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544,
   and `workspace/volume-09-batch-0004/PROMPT.md`.** Three further live pointers were corrected in the same pass because
   they were the same fault wearing different clothes: the *Where Volume 9 actually stands* block, the cold-start
   reading-order bullet (which was still sending a reader to ch 478 as the live position), and the closing pointer in
   `state/chapter-summaries.md`. **The historical blocks that name Batch 0003 from inside Batch 0002's own record are
   left exactly as they were written — they were true when written and are not headers.**
5. **F5 — two duplicated paragraphs, each in two adjacent chapters.** `They would have to ask her.` stood at
   `chapter-0512.md:27` and `chapter-0513.md:23`, and `He did not answer that either.` at `chapter-0515.md:99` and
   `chapter-0516.md:41`. **ch 513 now reads *And a space with nothing in it only ever gets written on by the one who
   comes and asks for it*, and ch 515 now reads *He put his hand flat on the trough and left it there, and he gave her
   back not one word of it.*** In each pair the instance that carries a beat survives — ch 512's first statement of the
   finding, and ch 516's, which answers `He did not answer.` two paragraphs above it and therefore needs its *either*.
   **The sentence either way carried the same meaning and no beat, figure or silence was touched.**
6. **F6 — one authorially-revealing section heading.** `chapter-0518.md:81` read `## The Question He Goes Looking For`,
   which names the frame scene's purpose from outside it — the class the Volume 7 close recorded at `Mouth Five` and
   `Mouth Six`. **It now reads `## The One Question`**, which is a thing in the room and not a note from the drafting
   brief. Its two sentences and the scene under it are untouched.

says twelve mornings five times.** F2's corrected row only closes if she stood twelve, because 1,545 through 1,556 is

is the whole of it — one word, in an inventory list, with nothing after it disturbed. **Left alone it would have been
the one place in the batch where the state file and the prose disagreed about how long she stood at a wall.**

## Volume 9 Batch 0004 (ch 524–538) — established facts, carried to the volume close

**Day 1,562 to day 1,598, thirty-seven days, pressure *surviving being kept*.** She is nineteen, Severance 3, three
stages past the ceiling, four fingers and a thumb, right arm open four days in five, eight of eleven holds, four counts
of breath everywhere, the open place low behind her fourth rib, no wages, no place, and the road behind her gone.

1. **The room is nine feet by eleven against the west face of the wall** — boards, a chair she will not sit in, a table,
   a blank book she will not take, a bed frame with nothing on it, a shelf with a jug and a folded blanket, a window
   facing west, a chimney that draws — **and it is hers for thirty-seven days because there is nowhere else, which is
   not the same as holding.** She sleeps on the boards on purpose and goes out of the opening at the fourth hour and
   does not knock.
2. **The paper is made, not taken.** A blanket off the shelf, three hundred and one sheets in three days counted in nine
   passes with the left hand, three square at first and nine square in nine hundred and six by the standard set on the
   second day; two folded books, soot ink, a date and a place on the first leaf. **The blank book on the table is never
   touched for writing.**
3. **Wei Ran carries her once, west, for nine coppers a head said out loud four times**, the bar in the cart and not in
   the rate, twenty-two miles in two days and a half. Eight coppers unchased and nine unchased with them. A trade, a
   team, a rate and an opinion, nothing else.
4. **The keeper says no.** He has none to sell and none to give; forty sheets on a shelf and none of them hers to ask
   for. She gives him nothing, asks him to keep nothing, leaves with nothing. **A thing that is kept is not a thing that
   is owed.** Three names in his book under the ladder in nineteen years, put there by himself, no office having asked.
5. **The rule in full, at ch 530**: a name goes in when a person gets to the top of a realm with nothing to take them
   past it, once, as kept or as lost, older than she is. Stated without persuading her of anything, never explained why.
6. **Structural mouth five of five, Shuyin's, at ch 531, in five short sentences across beats, never one speech** — not
   a place to join, not a punishment, a record of the ones the bound took, accurate, the accuracy the whole of it —
   **and once, the sentence he is wrong about, that a person on his book is better kept than a person off it, which he
   does not know is wrong about.** She cannot correct him and does not try.
7. **The Hunter, once, at ch 531**, nine feet off, notebook shut, and she asks him whether a person written as lost is
   safer than a person not written at all. **He says he does not know, and then says the other thing: that she asked
   him, and that this is the second question anybody has ever asked him that he would rather not answer.** His name is
   used once. His request stays pending.
8. **Entered either way; the word is hers.** No third place; he has outlived preferring and takes either. The page she
   gave him on the third is about her and not the line. One sentence about the door: the one who opened it stays on the
   wrong side of it.
9. **The demand at its maximum, at ch 533**: three stages past the top, nothing to take her past it, no other room to be
   written in — and the twenty-second at the ninth hour. She could write her own line with his brush and puts it back,
   because a record kept by the person it is about is not a record.
10. **The survival, at ch 534–535, in her own hand where his cannot take it out.** Four sentences, written on the
    eighteenth at the ninth hour, read back to him out loud on the twentieth in the ordinary voice — and she refuses
    kept, once, out loud. **She is entered. She is not kept. It cannot be un-written and it does not un-copy her ch 474
    page.**
11. **One line and a date, at ch 536, the twenty-second, in his own hand, and the word is *lost*.** He shuts the book.
    He walks her as far as a man who cannot come back across can come; she goes the last eleven feet alone, goes in,
    and he does not follow. **He is not thanked anywhere, and no chapter writes a scene in which he is thanked.**
12. **The road walked west one last time, at ch 537**, twenty-two miles in two days, past the gate, the crossing, the
    lane, the bench not stopped at and the store not entered — walked exactly as fast as always, going nowhere she has
    been. Severance 3's cost carried and shown, not re-derived.
13. **The book left on the stone under the ladder, at ch 538, day 1,598, at the ninth hour, in daylight, in front of
    three people, unasked and unkept-for.** Beside his book with an inch of stone between. **He has come west, twenty-two
    miles in three days, and stands nine miles west of the only room in which what he keeps is kept; the wall is still
    open behind him and the table stands in it with nothing on it.** She says his name once, in the open, with
    witnesses. **The last line of the volume is his answer, and nothing after it.**

### Readings taken against the outline

Per the drafting card's own instruction, the batch's six pinned readings, all kept on the page: **the demand at the
wall and never in the room, with his book on a plank table in the opening and him on his own side throughout, and no
chapter puts him on her side** · **the room nine by eleven for thirty-seven days, a place somebody lets her have and
not a place that holds** · **ch 531 his only mouth, five sentences he shuts the book on and a sixth that is the one wrong one, and *Shuyin* never in his own
mouth** · **he correct throughout and wrong about exactly one sentence** · **ch 536 one line and a date, the word
*lost*, the shut book, and the walk ending at the jamb with her last eleven feet alone and no thanks** · **ch 538 he
comes west out of the far ground down eleven miles of long road and along nine days of road and stands at the foot of
the keeper's ladder when she says the name, the wall still open behind him — and nothing in the batch explains the
wall.** No other departure.

departure day, both dark; *nine hundred yards east* for a hundred · **ch 537** — *six weeks* of gate trips for two
months; *the third* twice for the sixteenth; *thirteen weeks* for three months; *five days ago* for the eighteenth,
cut; *five days ago* for the clay, re-dated to the eighteenth of the month that has gone; the hut light *for the first
time since the eighth*, re-hung as dark the second night; *two days ago* for four · **ch 538** — the wall four hundred
yards east of a door eleven feet wide, re-hung as a wall east of her, still open.

## Volume 9 Batch 0004 — REVIEW REPAIR (applied on the page; findings from `logs/volume-09-batch-0004.review.log`)

**Five files touched, seven findings closed, twenty-two individual faults, and no beat, day, title, stage, bracket,
irreversible decision or planned event moved.** The batch was not re-drafted; the reviewer called the batch strong and the
prose it found strong is untouched except where a number was wrong. Twelve of the fifteen chapter files are
byte-identical to what the reviewer read.

- **R1 (high) — ch 531, the counts in the chapter that is about counting.** *Eleven words* for
  `“What is the book for,”` is **five** — at the opening frame and again at the reckoning at the close of the day;
  *nine words* for the Hunter's question is **fourteen**. Two more of the same class that the review did not list are
  closed with them: *four words* for `“It is not a place to join,”` is **seven**, twice, and *five words* for
  `“The record is accurate.”` is **four**. Every figure now matches the quoted line sitting above it.
- **R2 (high) — ch 536, the span.** The demand opens on the seventh, ch 536 is the twenty-second, and the span is
  **fifteen days** — the figure ch 530:109 already stated and did not propagate. Six elapsed uses corrected: what he
  did to her across it · the days of carrying the book across a hundred yards of flat · the sentence she has thought of
  more than any other he said · the last of the most consequential days of her life, twice · and the dirt his boots
  flattened. **The four `nine days of road` instances are untouched: that is the distance measure the book uses
  throughout the batch, not the same number, and it is left as it stands.**
- **R3 (medium) — ch 536, the speech counts, corrected with R2 because they sit in the same paragraphs.** The entry is
  **twenty-seven words and two words**, not *nine words and eleven words*; and he had said **thirty-two words of it**
  across the whole chapter, not nine.
- **R4 (medium) — ch 533, the dates.** *The first of the sixteenth*, twice, is a construction the manuscript does not
  use — the forms are *the first of a month* and *on the sixteenth*. **On the sixteenth** she picked up the brush. The
  second instance sat three clauses after the same paragraph had already dated the decision to the seventeenth, so the
  date is cut and what is left is the quarter of an hour and the line the brush goes back down on.
- **R5 (medium) — the `Twenty-two days` line at ch 536, now tracked rather than orphaned.** The prose is unchanged and
  deliberately unexplained: the narration marks it as the only thing he said that day she cannot place in nine hundred
  years, and she has not gone over it. **It read as an error only because nothing carried it.** It is now written up at
  `state/open-threads.md` §*Volume 9 Batch 0004 (ch 524–538)* item 8, with the instruction not to resolve it in a later
  repair pass and not to retcon it into a figure already on the page; marked OPEN in the ch 536 row of
  `state/chapter-summaries.md`; added to `workspace/volume-09-close/PROMPT.md` as close-audit item 8 and to the close's
  required continuity list, where it is listed as **still unplaced and still unanswered.**
- **R6 (low) — ch 531, the clock and the five.** The hours ran ninth → second → fifth, against the order the reader meets
  them; they now run **second, fifth, ninth**. The opening frame called the wrong sentence *one of those five* while the
  chapter delivered five, shut the book, and then gave a sixth; the frame now reads **six sentences over seven hours, and
  the sixth of them is the wrong one**, and the five-sentence close is written as what she counts and does not ask past,
  so the sixth lands as a beat instead of a contradiction. **Twelve days**, from the tenth, for the run to the
  twenty-second.
- **R7 (low) — two density and weight findings.** Ch 528's *a quarter of an hour because there was nothing else* repeated
  the reason four lines after *there is nothing to be done at a post* had given it; the reason is cut and the sweep
  stands. Ch 533's nineteen `nothing`es are left alone — they are *nothing to take them past it*, the argument of the
  chapter. **Ch 538 gains one section, *Five Days Of It*:** the four sentences she read him on the twentieth, the five
  days in which he has said one word about none of them, and her refusal to open that in the open in front of three
  people at the post. No event added, no beat moved, the last line unchanged — **1,748 → 1,994.**

**Measured after the repair:** body prose, method B, **28,961**, average ~1,931, inside the 27,000–30,000 band · per file
524 1,907 · 525 2,005 · 526 1,869 · 527 2,039 · 528 1,812 · 529 2,001 · 530 1,885 · 531 2,044 · 532 1,865 · 533 2,135 ·
534 1,643 · 535 1,820 · 536 1,909 · 537 2,033 · 538 1,994 · **0** paragraphs over 90 words, longest **89** · **538**
headings, **zero** collisions · **0** blockquotes, code blocks, tables and horizontal rules · **0** straight quotes and
**0** straight apostrophes · **0** bracketed layer lines in fifteen chapters, the volume's budget of three in sixty still
spent at ch 493, ch 508 and ch 523 · `about` 87 · `whole of` 39 · bare `eleven` 52 · `Shuyin` 1, in her mouth ·
`Ledger` `Warden` `Yuan` `Ning Wu` `Feng` `Anchor` `Lung` `Sisterhood` `Scribe` `bargain` `appointment` `budget`
`Wall writ` `field agent` all **0** — the review's single `Scribe` hit is the substring inside *describe*.

**Still true after the repair, re-checked on the page:** him on her side of the opening **never**, in fifteen chapters ·
the demand made at the wall and never in the room, in daylight, the book on plank table, him on his own side throughout ·
**he is not thanked anywhere** · no page of his book read out loud · the ch 267 surface answer never re-voiced · the
Hunter's request pending · the ten parts not given · no harem and nothing a romance · no new final enemy.

sixty chapters against `outline/volume-09.md`, **wrote no chapter prose, repaired nothing, edited no outline, and did not
open `outline/ending.md`.** Full record at `workspace/volume-09-close/SUMMARY.md`. Every figure below is re-derived from
the chapters at this close with the method in the same sentence as the number.

   · 1,544 · 1,574 · 1,604**, and **no chapter in ch 479–538 names a weekday, a calendar month or a week.**

   3 at day 1,561 (ch 523), the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544**. Intervals: 1,412 − 1,291 = **121** · 1,488 −
   1,412 = **76** · 1,561 − 1,488 = **73** · 1,561 − 1,412 = **149** first to third · 1,561 − 1,291 = **270** ceiling to

8. **The entry.** **She is entered and she is not kept.** ch 536 — the word is *lost*, in his hand, four letters, four
   seconds, under a date; the line is in and it stays in and it does not come out. ch 535 — *I am not going to be kept*,
   seven words, once, said out loud at a table in daylight.
9. **The record.** Eight leaves made out of a blanket in nine days; a date and a place on the first leaf, four sentences
   on the second, six empties that are hers. **On a flat stone under a ladder nine miles west at ch 538, beside the
   keeper's book with an inch of stone between them, unasked and unkept-for, in front of three people.** **The reason for
   the wall is recorded where he cannot reach it, for the first time in nine hundred years.**
10. **The walls stay.** ch 538:91 — the wall east of her is still open and the table stands in it with nothing on it.

11. **Shuyin, alive, old, not thanked, and never in his own mouth.** `Shuyin` **2** in body prose, ch 498:27 through a
    shut door and ch 538:117 in her mouth with three witnesses. **The word `thank` occurs 22 times in body prose across sixteen chapters — `thank` 18, `thanked` 3, `thanking` 1 —
    and not one of them is a thank to him; ch 538:123 says it outright.**
12. **The Hunter, twice and only twice**, ch 491 and ch 531, nine feet off, notebook shut, **and she asks him both
    times.** His request is still pending at ch 538. `Wall writ` **0**, `field agent` **0**.
13. **Yuan's position at the close.** **Readable without her and therefore findable** (ch 272) · **held and therefore
    sendable** (ch 338) · **answerable since ch 401, and this is the volume where somebody used it against him** · **he
    opened a wall from the inside once at ch 518 and is on the wrong side of it for the rest of the volume** · **his
    hand is still degrading and the degradation is carried, not re-derived** · **no figure of what is left of him is
    stated on any page**, and `Yuan` does not appear in body prose once in ch 479–538.
14. **The silence.** Counted from day 1,256, which is ch 462: **258 days at ch 516** (1,514 − 1,256) · **265 at ch 518**
    (1,521 − 1,256) · **273 at ch 520** (1,529 − 1,256), when he says *It was mine*, four words, the first to her since
    ch 471. **The frame scene at ch 518 is away from her hearing and is not counted against the silence.**
15. **The closing image and the closing question.** **A book of her own on a flat stone under a ladder, in daylight, in
    front of three people, with his name said out loud at a person instead of into flat ground — and his answer, which
    is the last line of the volume.** **The one thing the volume leaves open on purpose is two words he says at a jamb
    at ch 536 and that she never asks: *“Twenty-two days.”*** It is not resolved anywhere in ch 479–538 and it is not
    retconned into any figure already on the page.

## Volume 9 close — REVIEW REPAIR

**The Volume 9 close recorded twenty-six findings and repaired none. This section is what a repair pass did about them,
and it is written by the phase that wrote `logs/volume-09-close.review.log`'s six findings into action.** Findings and
file/line numbers are the close's, from `workspace/volume-09-close/SUMMARY.md`; every one was re-verified against the page
before it was touched and every fix was re-measured after. **Twenty-nine chapter files were opened and twenty-eight of them were edited; the twenty-ninth, ch 537, was read for
B2 and needed nothing. Not one beat, day
anchor, title, stage, bracket, structural mouth, irreversible decision or planned event moved, and at ch 536 the
*Twenty-two days* line was not touched.**

### A. The word-count tic — eleven claims in nine chapters, all eleven now reproduce

**The close's instruction was that the class is the finding and not the ten rows, so the whole family was re-measured and
not only the ten named.** Sixteen *N words* claims stand in ch 479–538; six reproduced before this pass and ten did not.
All ten are corrected, and **A11 was found by the re-measurement and was not in the close's table.**

| # | File | The claim | Was | Now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A1 | ch 491:71 | *That was __ words* over *Then it is not that.* | three | **five** |
| A2 | ch 491:103 | *put to her as plainly as that, in __ words* over *It is not a thing anybody asks for* | eleven | **eight** |
| A3 | ch 497:117 | *she got __ words out of it* over *“Two,” / “How long between them.”* | nine | **five** |
| A4 | ch 502:89 | *it took __ words* over *Four times somebody else wrote me down. The fifth time I said it to him.* | eleven | **fifteen** |
| A5 | ch 508:15 | *__ words in the ordinary voice, once* over *“What am I at.”* | nine | **four** |
| A6 | ch 519:53 | *Eleven words, once* over *“I know it is you in there,”* | eleven | **seven** |
| A7 | ch 532:15 | *it was __ words* over *“What happens if I do not choose either word.”* | eleven | **nine** |
| A8 | ch 533:75 | *That is __ words and it is a date and an hour and a place* over three sentences | six | **eleven** |
| A9 | ch 535:51 | *That is __ words and one of them is the same word* | nine | **eleven** |
| A10 | ch 534:23 | *That is __ words* — **no such utterance exists in the chapter** | six | **recast, not renumbered: *That is what it took to learn a hand she has never had.*** |
| A11 | ch 531:133 | *it is __ words long* — **no such utterance exists on the page** | eleven | **recast: *it has never once been reduced to a number of words*** |

**The near-miss at ch 491:23 — *she got nine words into it and threw the rest away*, about a draft she abandoned and not
about the line she said — is left exactly as it stands, because the page says so in the same sentence, and the six claims
that already reproduced (ch 531 at five, seven, fourteen and four; ch 535 at seven; ch 536 at twenty-seven and two; ch
536:113 at thirty-two) were not touched at all.**

### B. The calendar and the staging — four defects, five files

- **B1, ch 532 and ch 533 — resolved in favour of the chapter's own frame.** ch 532 opens *She spent the thirteenth,
  fourteenth and fifteenth of a month* and its two `seventeenth`s contradicted that frame. **Both are now the fifteenth,
  and ch 533:61 — *She had asked him for that on the seventeenth*, which pointed forward to a day its own chapter had not
  reached — is now the fifteenth too.** The chain reads: she asks on the fifteenth, and on the sixteenth he does not soften
  it. **ch 533's sixteenth, at lines 3, 11, 19 and 71, did not move, and ch 533's own seventeenth at lines 91, 107, 119,
  131 and 143 did not move.** The close named one file for this class; the fix needed two, because ch 533:61 was the same
  error pointing the other way.
- **B2, ch 536 §*The Two Days After* — observed from somewhere she can be, not moved.** ch 536 had her going out four
  times a day for two days at the opening on the twenty-third and the twenty-fourth while ch 537 has her **out of the far
  ground at the fourth hour of the twenty-third.** The section now says she was on a made road for both days and did not
  come back, and it marks the observer: on the twenty-fourth at the fourth hour **she was at a crossing eleven miles off it
  and did not know the light was out and has not asked.** **The twenty-third, the twenty-fourth, the dark hut on both
  nights and the departure at the fourth hour of the twenty-third are all still on the page, and the pull-back — the
  narrator stating what is behind her — is the convention ch 537:121 already uses when it says *dark now for the second
  night*.** ch 537 was not opened.
- **B3, ch 524:3.** *a month that began on the seventeenth of the one before it* → **the first**. Under
  `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` day 1,562 is the nineteenth of the month beginning 1,544, and no month in this world begins
  on the seventeenth. **Day 1,562 did not move and ch 524's own thirty-seven at line 7 did not move.**
- **B4, ch 525:3.** *thirty-six days left in it* → **thirty-three**. The chapter opens on the twenty-second, which is day
  1,565, and 1,598 − 1,565 = 33 on ch 524's own exclusive subtraction. Thirty-six was ch 524's figure, counted from the
  nineteenth. **The thirty-seven does not move.**

### C. The retired constructions and the allowances — four defects, seventeen files, two different answers

**C1, ch 502 — the one reference to non-forgiveness is cut, and nothing else is.** `outline/volume-09.md` items at §*Starting
relationships* and §*Required continuity facts* 14 both give the allowance as **0** and state the strongest form: *the
subject is not mentioned once in sixty chapters.* The line was *and she has said out loud on a made road that she does not
forgive and does not ask to be forgiven for any of it.* **It is now *and she said out loud on a made road, once, that it
is not over and that it is not hers to finish.*** The fact travels — the account is still carrying and she said so out loud, years ago, on a made road — and the subject does not appear. **Nothing at ch 466 or ch 474 was opened, nothing was
softened, and ch 466's bill and ch 474's refusal on the record are exactly as they were.**

**C2, ch 499, ch 501, ch 505 — the three instances stay and the budget is amended, because the budget was the thing that
was wrong.** `Nobody asked them.` (ch 499:69, the rule has never asked anybody) and `Nobody asked you to.` (ch 505:53,
the keeper was never asked for the stone) are the volume's argument **in dialogue**, and ch 501:93's *There is no column
and there is no board and there is no rate* is a three-part anaphora doing the work of the chapter. **All three stand,
unreworded, and the construction budget in the section above now records 3 against an allowance of 0 instead of claiming 0
while instances stand.** Batch 0002's record, which claimed *the retired seven phrases **0 as phrases***, is wrong and is
corrected in place in that record.

**C3, ch 510, ch 512, ch 513, ch 514, ch 523 — recast, five instances, two retired tokens.** The three *about four
seconds* at ch 510:45, ch 513:31 and ch 514:21 are one measure — the fourth count of breath coming back on a reach — and
the two *eleven weeks* at ch 512:7 and ch 523:31 are one interval. **The *about* is gone from all three (the measure is
the same four seconds at ch 537:35 and ch 537:95), ch 512's interval is now *since she first stood in front of it*, and
ch 523's is now *three months*, which is the figure ch 537:55 already publishes.** Both retired tokens are now **0 in
sixty**.

**C4, sixteen authorial-scaffolding lines in fourteen chapters — cut, not recorded as a house move.** The outline's
allowance is 0 and Volume 8 measured 0 across fifty-eight chapters. Every narrator move that pointed at the book as an
object is recast into a move that points at her: *she wants that on the page* → *that is* or *she wants that said* or *she
is going to say all three*; *the thing the chapter is named for* → **and then she kept still**; *he has been nine feet off
for four volumes* → **since the first time she saw him**; *the reason this is the chapter* → **the reason the eleventh of
a month is what it is**; *the reason the chapter is what it is* → **the reason the twentieth of a month is what it is**;
*this volume* → **the rest of this**; *wants it in the page in her own words* → **wants it said in her own words** (ch 505
and ch 530, two instances the close's table did not name). **Sixteen lines in fourteen files: ch 480, 485, 490, 491, 494,
496, 505, 507, 508, 530, 531, 533, 535 ×2 and 538 ×2. The seven in-world uses of *on the page* — ch 509 in her mouth,
ch 516, ch 518 ×2, ch 531:81, ch 536 ×2 — were left exactly as they stand, because they are the world's paper and not the
novel's.**

### D. Distances and files

- **D1, ch 531:65** — *four said out of the dark on the sixteenth of a month at a hundred yards* → **at seven hundred
  yards.** The figure is ch 525:11's and ch 520:71's, and the hundred yards is the hut's distance from the wall (ch
  524:79), which is how the error got in. **One figure, one line, one file.**
- **D2** — Batch 0002's claim of *0 exact duplicate paragraphs* is **wrong and stands corrected in place**: eleven
  cross-chapter duplicate paragraphs of six words or more stand in ch 479–538, of which two have both ends inside Batch
  0002. **Five are deliberate refrains, three are ch 534/535's four sentences reproduced because she reads them back to
  him, and none of them is a fault.**
- **D3 — 58 files given the trailing newline they lacked.** ch 479–538 is now 60 of 60.

### E. The recorded figures — corrected at the source, not only in this record

**`workspace/volume-09-close/PROMPT.md` published pre-repair figures and would have re-imported them.** Its body-prose
row now reads the page as it stands and carries a note saying which figures it used to carry. **All four state files that
quoted 113,790 / 27,725 / 28,532 / 28,961 are corrected or superseded by this section; the pre-repair figures are left in
place where they are a record of what was measured at the time, and the corrected set travels beside them.**

**The +21 is the whole of the repair's cost and it is not prose: ch 536's *The Two Days After* is thirty words longer
because it now has to say that she was on a road and did not know what the light was doing, and ch 512's interval is six
words longer because it no longer carries a retired figure. Nothing was padded to reach it.**

### What this repair deliberately did not do

- **It did not re-plan, re-outline or preview anything.** No outline file was opened for editing and `outline/ending.md`
  was not opened at all.
- **It did not touch a title, a stage day, a bracket, a structural mouth, an irreversible step or the *Twenty-two days*
  line.**
- **It did not repair F1 or F2, because neither is a page defect.** **F1** — `outline/volume-09.md` allows Wei Ran *at
  most two cards in Volume 9, one in Batch 0002 and one in Batch 0004*, and she is on the page in three chapters (ch 497,
  ch 505, ch 527; 22 occurrences on word boundaries). **Batch 0002 therefore carries one card more than the allowance, and
  no outline may be edited by any phase, so this is carried to Volume 10 planning to carry or to forgive.** **F2** — the
  outline pins *four or five short sentences each* across five mouths and ch 484's mouth delivers nine; four of five hold
  and the fifth is a structural mouth, which the repair order forbids touching. **Both are recorded and carried.**
- **It did not renumber ch 488's or ch 502's *forty words*.** The bench sentence is counted as forty words in eleven
  places across ch 502, 509, 510, 512, 513, 516, 517, 518 and 529 and the claim stands as a carried figure of the
  manuscript; the close did not count it as one of the sixteen *N words* claims and this repair did not widen the class
  into it, **because changing it would mean changing the refusal entry itself, which is one of the volume's four
  irreversible steps.**
- **It did not write a `.done` marker.** That is the controller's.

## Volume 10 planned — decisions, allowances and the two defects carried

**Written by the Volume 10 planning phase, which wrote `outline/volume-10.md` and
`workspace/volume-10-batch-0001/PROMPT.md` and no chapter prose. `outline/series.md`, `outline/ending.md` and
`outline/volume-09.md` were read and not edited. Every day below is derived under `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1`.**

- **Range and goal:** ch 539–600, sixty-two chapters. *In this volume, Ning Yan must find her brother before the
  Warden's list closes for good, or Ning Wu stays a name on a page for another decade.*
- **Stages:** Severance 4 at day 1,629 (ch 556; twenty-sixth of the month beginning 1,604) · Severance 5 at day
  1,697 (ch 572; fourth of the month beginning 1,694) · Severance 6 at day 1,772 (ch 588; nineteenth of the month
  beginning 1,754). Intervals 1,629 − 1,561 = **68** · 1,697 − 1,629 = **68** · 1,772 − 1,697 = **75** ·
  first-to-third 1,772 − 1,629 = **143** · volume span day 1,599 to day 1,830 = **232 inclusive**. Costs named
  once each: uncountable where she stands · untold where she follows · unkeeping what she finds.
- **Brackets:** three in sixty-two, at ch 556, 572, 588, far-side house form; Batch 0001 allowance **0 in
  sixteen**.
- **Him:** sixth frame scene at ch 584, present tense, away from her hearing; no wall opened; no figure of him;
  `Ledger` 0; non-forgiveness subject 0 in sixty-two.
- **Shuyin:** shuts the book at ch 596–597, no page read, no name read, nothing shown; Anchor never named;
  alive, old, not thanked; not the final enemy. **Hunter:** at most twice, she asks every time, request pending.
  **Wei Ran:** at most two cards (Batches 0001, 0004). **Ning Wu:** want-only until Batch 0004; in person once at
  ch 598–599; never in Shuyin's mouth, never on a list page.
- **Midpoint:** closing day day 1,802 (nineteenth of the month beginning 1,784) named at ch 569–570.
  ***Twenty-two days* OPEN, unplaced; no card equates it to a dated interval** — it is a count out of his keeping
  she has not earned yet.
- **Density targets with Volume 9's reals beside them:** `about` average under 7.0 a chapter (Vol 9: 422, 7.0)
  · `whole of` under 80 volume-wide (Vol 9: 193) · bare `eleven` under 200 (Vol 9: 218). Retired seven phrases:
  allowance 3 volume-wide, no fourth.
- **F1 carried:** Wei Ran on three chapters against two allowed; Volume 10's allowance states the true figure
  beside it. **F2 carried:** ch 484's mouth nine against four-or-five; Volume 10 mouths run three to seven and
  an overrun is a close finding, not a mid-draft defect. **Neither is forgiven and neither touches the page.**
- **Batch 0001:** ch 539–554, days 1,599–1,628, pressure *asking what else is on it*, irreversible step the
  asking at ch 553–554, 27,000–30,000 words of body prose on method B.

## Volume 10 planning — REVIEW REPAIR (applied on the page; findings from `logs/volume-10-planning.review.log`)

**The review raised five defects against `outline/volume-10.md` and one of them against the Batch 0001 card. All five
are cleared in place. No chapter was opened and no chapter prose exists; no day moved, no stage moved, no chapter
number moved, no cost was re-worded, no mouth was moved or re-argued, and `outline/series.md`, `outline/ending.md`
and `outline/volume-09.md` were not touched. Every figure written below was re-measured against the manuscript
before it was published.**

1. **The title figure, `outline/volume-10.md` construction budget.** The row read *longest new title 30
   characters* beside *538 `#` headings manuscript-wide*. Re-measured: **the longest title in the manuscript is 39
   characters — *The Question She Had Not Thought To Ask*, ch 426 — then 36 at ch 467 and 34 at ch 443.** Thirty is
   Volume 9's own longest, and it is a true figure of the wrong scope. **The row now names both, with the chapters
   beside them: Volume 9's new titles peak at 30, the manuscript's peak is 39.**
2. **The 68-day interval, Severance 4.** The stage row called 1,629 − 1,561 **the gap from the asking to the first
   step past it.** It is not: **the asking is ch 553–554 at days 1,627–1,628 and the stage is the day after the
   terms are named, so the asking falls inside that sixty-eight and not after it.** The arithmetic was right and
   the label was wrong, and the row instructed the chapter to explain the label. **It now says the asking is inside
   the sixty-eight and that one day is all the asking gets.** No day changed.
3. **The word bands did not sum.** Three sixteen-chapter batches at 27,000–30,000 plus a fourteenth-chapter batch at
   25,500–29,000 is **106,500–119,000**, against a stated volume of 108,000–118,000 — a floor 1,500 out of reach and
   a ceiling 1,000 over. **Batch 0004 is now 27,000–28,000 and the four bands sum exactly: 108,000 at every floor and
   118,000 at every ceiling.** The per-chapter averages in the same paragraph were inherited from Volume 9's
   fifteen-chapter shape and were wrong for sixteen: **27,000–30,000 over sixteen chapters is 1,688–1,875, not
   1,800–2,000.** They are now published as measured, **with Volume 9's real pace of 1,849–1,933 a chapter beside
   them**, and the volume average is 1,742–1,903 over sixty-two. `workspace/volume-10-batch-0001/PROMPT.md` carried
   the same wrong average and is corrected in the same sentence as its own band.
4. **Ch 572 and ch 578 were the same beat.** The Severance 5 cost row ended *the chapter is about walking past the
   place she was told to*, and Batch 0003's midpoint at ch 578 was *she walks past the place she was told to and
   keeps walking* — one beat six chapters apart inside one batch. **They are now two beats: ch 572 is the gift
   (somebody hands her a place, and in the same motion the telling takes the road out of it, so the cost is paid the
   moment it is in her hands), and ch 578 is the road.** Both the stage row and the escalation sequence say so, and
   each says which chapter the other one is not, so a drafter cannot merge them. The ch 598–599 echo of the walking
   is Batch 0004's payoff of ch 578 and **stands as it stands.**
5. **Ch 544's structural mouth was anchored to an event already in the past.** The mouth was a carter *who watered a
   horse at the post trough **while** a book was put down*, and the book went down at **ch 538, day 1,598 — nine days
   and six chapters before ch 544 at day 1,607.** The carter's presence is sound; ch 538 puts him at the post with two
   others and he saw it. **The mouth is now marked retrospective in both files — the outline's mouth row, a new note
   under the mouth table, and the Batch 0001 card at ch 544 — and it states that the book has not moved since the
   ninth hour of the twenty-fifth of the month, that no chapter in Volume 10 puts it on that stone again, and that
   she takes it off at ch 563. That is the only hand that moves it.** The Batch 0001 opening section now carries the
   same anchor so a drafter reading the prompt alone cannot stage the putting down twice.

**Two facts this repair pins for the drafting phases, because they are the ones a card could get wrong:** **ch 572
is the gift and ch 578 is the road, and no card merges them** · **the book on the stone is put down once, at ch 538,
and moves once, at ch 563.** Everything the Volume 10 planning section above states is otherwise unchanged, including
the three stages at days 1,629 · 1,697 · 1,772, the intervals 68 · 68 · 75, the midpoint's closing day 1,802, the
bracket budget of three, and *Twenty-two days* still OPEN and unplaced.
